## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a consistent branded system, a clear agenda, and section dividers that make a 49-page release briefing easy to navigate, and the strongest slides (11, 20, 35) use clear titles, hierarchy and purposeful visuals. The consequential weaknesses are legibility and density: slide 11 has a tiny itemised price list inside the bars, slides 38 and 41 to 44 rely on unreadable screenshots with thin labels, and slides 25, 27 and 37 carry very small text and callouts. The Group Benefits run (slides 41 to 44) repeats one template with little explanation, so the feature story fades into screenshot tours, and a few slides, such as the pricing tiles on 10 and 12 and the agenda-style dividers, are packed or filler-like.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the editions slide 11 structure of paired bars with a value/price multiple, the problem framing on slide 20 with its single stat, and the clear title-plus-subtitle-plus-benefit-list pattern of slide 35.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge or crop the screenshots on slides 38 and 41 to 44 to the one relevant region and add a one-line takeaway per slide, and raise the minimum text size on slides 11, 12, 25 and 27.

**What would change the score:** I would cut or merge the repeated Group Benefits slides and the empty-looking section dividers (14, 15, 17) to tighten the route and give each remaining feature one readable proof visual.

_Confidence medium. 49 of 49 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide for the New Financial Services Cloud Editions and Winter '27 (264) release, followed by a thank-you slide listing customer groups, a forward-looking statements disclaimer, and an agenda. It moves through a pricing and editions section that compares Core, Advanced and Max tiers and bundles, then covers banking service and origination updates, wealth management features for advisors including the Agentic Advisor, Run My Day and Enhanced Client Details, insurance claims intake and financial authority controls, group benefits capabilities, and a sneak peek at agentic underwriting. The closing sections are a set of upcoming features and a final thank-you slide. Several slides use side-by-side comparisons of value and price, and the wealth section frames its problem with a short sequence of pain points before showing the product. Slides worth studying include the editions value comparison on slide 11, the advisor problem framing on slide 20, the claims intake flow on slide 35, and the group benefits challenge and solution split on slide 40.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the Salesforce Financial Services Cloud deck have?**

The Salesforce deck has 49 slides. The agenda on slide 4 lists six sections that structure the deck, from welcome and introduction through the sneak peek of coming features.

**What does the Salesforce deck say about Financial Services Cloud edition pricing?**

Slide 8 lists Core starting at $325 per user/per month, Advanced starting at $500 per user/per month, and Max starting at $700 per user/per month. Slide 10 gives the Sales & Service editions at $350, $525 and $725 per user/per month.

**What does the Salesforce deck say about the Agentic Advisor for wealth management?**

Slide 22 marks the Agentic Advisor as GA Now and describes it as a way to reimagine high-touch relationship management. The same slide lists related enhancements such as Meeting Concierge, Run My Day and Enhanced Client Details.

**What value-to-price ratios does the Salesforce deck show for its bundled editions?**

Slide 11 says the new FSC bundling delivers significantly more value vs earlier editions. It shows value-to-price ratios of 1.5x for Sales & Service Core, 1.7x for Sales & Service Advanced, and 1.9x for Sales & Service Max.

### Slide 2

**Text**

Thank / you / Customers / Trailblazers / Veterans / Employees / Partners / Community

**Layout**

Centred composition on a flat light-blue field. The large two-line headline 'Thank / you' sits in the centre, with a Salesforce cloud logo above it. Six circular photos with blue pill labels surround the headline: Customers top-left, Trailblazers top-right, Veterans right, Employees left, Partners bottom-right and Community bottom-centre-left. The Astro mascot stands bottom-right, overlapping the headline's lower edge. There is no footer, slide number or margin-bound text grid.

**Visuals**

Six circular photographs, each with a dark-blue pill label beneath it. (1) Customers: four people in front of a wall of colourful Crocs-style shoes, one holding a blue clog. (2) Trailblazers: a crowd at a conference, with a group selfie of smiling attendees wearing lanyards, cropped at top. (3) Veterans: a smiling woman in a cream jacket shaking hands with a woman in camouflage uniform, with a blurred background. (4) Employees: four young people around a wooden table in front of an orange and teal tiled wall, one holding a laptop. (5) Community: volunteers in navy 'volunteer' T-shirts packing boxes of food and supplies in an office with an exit sign. (6) Partners: five people in front of an 'Agentforce Partner Summit' backdrop with yellow confetti and a cartoon cat with sunglasses. Photos are circular crops, unboxed, with no overlay. Decorative 3D illustrations: a party-horn emoji face at upper left, a blue striped bear-like robot face with visor at top centre-right, two blue sparkle stars (one small near centre-top, one large blue wisp at top right) and the brown Astro mascot waving at lower right. Logo: the Salesforce cloud wordmark 'salesforce' in white on a #00A1E0 cloud at the centre top.

**Design**

Background: Flat solid colour #CFE9FE (light blue) across the full slide. Flat light-blue background #CFE9FE. Headline 'Thank you' in a geometric heavy sans-serif, close to Avant Garde Demi, weight demi-bold, colour navy #001E5B, set very large (about 25% of slide height per line). Pill labels in a geometric demi-bold sans-serif, white #FFFFFF, about 3% of slide height, on a vertical gradient from #0A4DB3 at top to #002D74 at bottom. Pills are fully rounded (radius half their height) with a soft drop shadow. Photos are circles with no border. Logo cloud #00A1E0 with white wordmark.

**Text**

Forward-looking statements
This presentation contains forward-looking statements about, among other things, trend analyses and statements

If any such risks or uncertainties materialize or if any of the assumptions prove incorrect, results or outcomes could differ materially from those expressed or implied by these forward-looking statements. The risks and uncertainties referred to above include those factors discussed in Salesforce’s reports filed from time to time with the Securities and Exchange Commission, including, but not limited to our ability to meet the expectations of our customers; uncertainties regarding AI technologies and their integration into our product offerings; the effect of evolving domestic and foreign government regulations; regulatory developments and regulatory investigations involving us or affecting our industry; our ability to successfully introduce new services and product features, including related to AI and Agentforce; our ability to execute our business plans; the pace of change and innovation and our ability to compete in the markets in which we participate; and our ability to maintain and enhance our brands.

**Layout**

Two-zone composition: a tall white rounded panel with a concave curved right edge occupies the left-centre (about x 4.7 to 40.5%, y 8.7 to 91%), holding the illustration. The text block sits left-aligned in the blue field to the right of the curve, starting at x 40.6%, stacked as a small two-line eyebrow above a large three-line statement. The Salesforce cloud logo sits top right at about x 90.5%, y 8.2%. No footer, no slide number, no date.

**Visuals**

Illustration cluster on the white panel, all 3D rendered in glossy blue and yellow: a fuzzy blue round mascot head with a black glossy visor/sunglasses and a light-blue highlight, at about x 7.6 to 20.9%, y 28 to 62%, overlapping the top-left corner of a solid blue (#0250D9) rounded card at x 9.4 to 24.2%, y 41.7 to 68%; a blue upward-right arrow icon (darker blue #0A5AE6) on that card near x 19 to 24%, y 44 to 51%; a light-blue (#00B3FF) square tile at x 25.6 to 32.4%, y 55.9 to 68% partly behind a translucent white search pill (white at 40% opacity) at x 16.8 to 30.2%, y 59.2 to 65%, which holds a small purple four-point sparkle icon in a white circle at its left end and a white rectangular field to the right; a brown-skinned clapping-hands emoji (3D) at x 26.5 to 32.2%, y 50.8 to 61.8%, overlapping the tile; small 3D sparkles (a yellow four-point sparkle at about x 16.7 to 19%, y 28.9 to 32%, a light-blue four-point sparkle at about x 18.9 to 22.1%, y 28.6 to 36%, a small light-blue sparkle at about x 11.3 to 13%, y 64 to 67%, and a clustered yellow sparkle pair beside the hands); a light-blue horizontal progress bar at x 9.4 to 24.2%, y 70.5 to 74.7% with a darker blue (#0250D9) filled segment covering about the left 55% of it; a large glossy yellow four-point star at the lower-left edge of the panel at x 0 to 11.3%, y 57.4 to 84.6%, partly bleeding off the slide edge. Lighting is soft and frontal with glossy highlights; no photograph. The Salesforce cloud logo at top right is light blue (#4DB8FF) with white lowercase wordmark 'salesforce'.

**Design**

Background is a saturated blue gradient: bright cobalt #0056E0 at the top, deepening to royal blue #0230A0 toward the right and lower-right, with a luminous cyan glow #00A3FF along the bottom edge. The white panel (#FFFFFF) has rounded corners of about 2% of slide width on the left and top/bottom corners and a smooth concave curve on its right edge, no shadow or border. Titles and body text are white #FFFFFF in AvantGardeDemiSFDC, a geometric sans-serif in demi weight, not italic, no capitals, normal letter spacing. The eyebrow 'Financial Services Cloud / New Editions' is set at about 5.2% of slide height with tight leading; the main statement 'One Platform. / More Value. / Less Complexity.' is about 8.9% of slide height with roughly 1.0 line spacing, lines spaced about 9.5% of slide height apart. Accents: #00B3FF light cyan, #0250D9 royal blue, #00B3FF tiles, yellow #FFC83D star and sparkles, purple #6B1F8A sparkle icon. No gradients on the panel, no line strokes, no outlines.

### Slide 6

**Text**

Your teams are spending / more time managing / software than serving / customers. / 14+ / Internal Systems / per institution are managed / through CRM middleware / Source: Market Reports World / salesforce

**Layout**

Centred headline block of four lines occupies the middle of the slide, with the text block centred horizontally around x 50 and the first line starting near y 25. A white circle with the stat sits lower right, with its caption block beneath it aligned to its centre; the Salesforce logo sits top right with a margin of about 8% from the top and 5% from the right. The source line is the bottom element of the right column, with no slide number visible.

**Visuals**

A white filled circle (#FFFFFF) at x 81.3-93.8, y 56.4-78.7 (about 12.5% of width) containing the text 14+ in dark blue #022AC0, set in a bold geometric sans. Light blue highlight rectangles (#90D0FE) sit behind the words 'spending', 'more time managing' and 'software', each a flat filled box with square corners. A Salesforce cloud logo in light blue (#4FB3F6 approx.) with white lettering 'salesforce' at top right, x 90.5, y 8.2, w 5.2, h 6.5. The slide background is a photographic-style blue gradient, not a photograph: brightest blue near the top centre and a glowing lighter cyan band along the bottom edge around x 50, darkening to deep blue at the left and right edges.

**Design**

Background is a radial-style blue gradient from about #0050E0 at top centre to #0038B8 at edges and a glow of #0A9CFF along the bottom centre. Headline text is white #FFFFFF on the blue for unhighlighted words and dark navy #001E5B for highlighted words. Headline typeface is AvantGardeDemiSFDC, a geometric sans-serif in demi-bold weight, size about 9.14% of slide height, left/centre aligned. Body caption text is SalesforceSans-Regular, a sans-serif in regular weight, white, about 2.26% of slide height; the caption title 'Internal Systems' is in AvantGardeDemiSFDC white about 2.82%. Stat numeral '14+' is AvantGardeDemiSFDC about 8% of slide height in #022AC0. Highlight fills #90D0FE have square corners and no shadow. No borders, no shadows on text.

### Slide 7

**Text**

From 12 vendors to one platform / Fragmented Buying Hazards / Multiple vendors, paperwork, integration & renewal risks / Domain extensions / Telephony & Messaging / Contracting, Maps Advanced / Origination / Forecasting / Conv. Intel / Outcome Based Agents / Analytics / Collaboration / GenAI / Data Platform / CRM (w/ support) / The New Way: All-Inclusive Single SKU / Maximum value, single contract, zero integration or maintenance overhead / Max / Digital Insurance & Digital Origination / Contact Center Dig., Workforce Mgmt. / Contracts, Maps Advanced / Advanced / Collections & Financial Recovery, / Insurance Brokerages / Sales Programs / Service & Sales Intelligence / Help Agent & Agentforce / Tableau Next / Slack / Agentforce / Data 360 / Industry Cloud (w/ Premier Success Plan) / Core / salesforce

**Layout**

Two-column comparison inside a large rounded near-white panel that spans most of the slide width. The title sits top-left at the slide's upper margin, spanning a single line. The left column (Fragmented Buying Hazards list, 12 rows) occupies roughly x 11.6 to 41.6 percent and the right column (The New Way tiers with 12 rows) occupies roughly x 45.7 to 88.5 percent; a narrow gap of about 4 percent separates them, with curly brace connectors bridging them. Text is left-aligned in both columns; the tier labels are left-aligned in their colour blocks. No footer, no slide number, no source line.

**Visuals**

Left: a stacked list of 12 white rows with thin grey dashed separators under a dark grey header block, titled Fragmented Buying Hazards with a subtitle; the rows are labelled Domain extensions, Telephony & Messaging, Contracting, Maps Advanced, Origination, Forecasting, Conv. Intel, Outcome Based Agents, Analytics, Collaboration, GenAI, Data Platform, CRM (w/ support). Centre: three grey-outlined curly brace connectors (dark grey #444444, about 0.15 percent stroke) join left row groups to tiers: the first brace spans Domain extensions through Origination and points to Max; the second spans Forecasting through Outcome Based Agents and points to Advanced; the third spans Analytics through CRM (w/ support) and points to Core. Right: three vertical tier blocks labelled Max (dark navy), Advanced (bright sky blue) and Core (pale blue), each with a small green spreadsheet-style grid icon beside the label; to the right, 12 white rows with dashed separators list the included products. Decorative: a white-and-blue astronaut robot mascot with sunglasses and a glowing jet trail, cropped at the panel's top-right edge and overlapping the header area; a blue Salesforce cloud logo with white lowercase wordmark salesforce at top right; a blue four-point sparkle star at lower left.

**Design**

Background: Solid light sky blue #CFE9FE covering the full slide. Pale sky-blue background with a lighter translucent panel (white at 40 percent opacity) with rounded corners and no shadow. Title in AvantGardeDemiSFDC, a geometric sans-serif demi weight, dark navy #002775 for From 12 vendors to and royal blue #023AC0 for one platform; title size about 5.9 percent of slide height. Headers are solid blocks with rounded top corners: dark grey #444444 with white bold sans text, and royal blue #023AC0 with white text. Body text is AvantGardeDemiSFDC in navy #002775, about 2.5 percent of slide height. Subtitles are italic in pale blue #90D0FE (left) and very pale cyan #E0F7FF (right). Row fills are #FEFEFD with thin grey dashed dividers. Tier blocks: Max #002775, Advanced #00B3FF, Core #90D0FE, with white labels in about 3 percent of slide height. Accent colours: navy, royal blue, sky blue and white. No gradients.

### Slide 8

**Text**

Core. Advanced. Max. / Core / Edition / starting at / $325 / per user/per month / Your foundation / AI, analytics, and collaboration built in / from day one. / everything in Core plus… / Slack Business+ with Slackbot / Tableau Next with Tableau Agent / Sales Emails / Premier Success Plan / Security Essentials / Data & AI / 500K Flex Credits/org / 20MB data storage/user / Advanced / Edition / starting at / $500 / per user/per month / From reactive to proactive / Intelligence, security, and industry tools / that replace separate contracts. / everything in advanced plus… / Casey, Help Agent with / outcome-Based Pricing / Sales Insights, Account Research / Sales Programs / Customer Signals Intelligence / Backup & Recovery, Archive / Data Detect, Security Center / *Insurance Brokerages, / Collections & Financial Recovery / Data & AI / 1M Flex Credits/org / 1GB data storage/user / Max / Edition / starting at / $700 / per user/per month / The complete picture / Unmetered AI, contact center, Digital / Insurance and Origination, everything in / one stack. / Coworker unmetered; All Sales, / Service, Industry Agent Templates / Momentum, Salesforce Contracts / Contact Center Digital / Workforce Management, Quality / Mgmt. / *Industry add-ons : Digital / Insurance & Digital Origination / Data & AI / 2.75M Flex Credits/org / 1GB data storage/user / *Base capability only. Seed credits to be included. Customers who wish to use at scale will need to purchase additional credits. Digital Origination is only available with FSC Sales Max and FSC Sales & Service Max

**Layout**

Three equal-width pricing columns of cards sit side by side across the middle of the slide, each card about 25% of slide width with even gutters, starting at about 23% of slide height and ending near 83%. The title Core. Advanced. Max. sits top-left at about 9% from the top, left-aligned at about 10% from the left edge, with the Salesforce cloud logo at top-right and a small icon left of the title. Each card has a white header box with the edition name and price above it, a white tagline box, a 'everything in' line, a bullet list, and a dark blue Data & AI button at the bottom. A footnote runs full-width along the bottom at about 97% from the top, left-aligned at about 1.5% from the left edge. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Three pricing cards with navy outlines and soft drop shadows. Each card has a gold coin badge with a four-point star at its lower right corner, overlapping the Data & AI button edge. Small colour icons precede the bullet items: Core card has a Slack hash logo, a Tableau logo, a blue face icon for Sales Emails, a blue person-with-check icon for Premier Success Plan, and a purple lightning bolt for Security Essentials. Advanced card has a blue face icon for Casey, a green square arrow icon for Sales Programs, a pink heart icon for Customer Signals Intelligence, a purple lightning bolt for Backup & Recovery, and a blue office building icon for Insurance Brokerages. Max card has a blue face icon for Coworker unmetered, a green square arrow icon for Momentum, a pink heart icon for Contact Center Digital, and a blue office building icon for Industry add-ons. A green rounded-square icon with a gear and grid sits left of the title. No photographs or charts are present.

**Design**

Background: Solid light blue #CFE9FE covering the whole slide.

### Slide 9

**Text**

Built-In value to power your Agentic Transformation
Native / Financial / Services / expertise, so / you get started / faster
AI in every / edition, / delivering value / from day one
Slack built in, / so teams work / where they / already are
Tableau, / delivering / insights where / work happens
Enterprise / platform and / security, built to / scale
Success included so you accelerate adoption and value
salesforce

**Layout**

Single slide with a full-bleed light blue background. The title sits top-left at about 9% from the top, with the Salesforce cloud logo at top-right. A row of five equal white cards is centred in the middle band, each with a circular icon disc overlapping its top edge, spaced evenly across the width with margins of about 4.5% on each side. A full-width translucent bar spans the lower band beneath the cards, holding one line of text with a person icon at its left. Four small yellow sparkles overlap the left and right edges. No footer or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Five app or product icons sit on pale translucent circular discs above the cards, left to right: a green spreadsheet-style grid icon with a gear (171 by 171 px image, about 3.7% by 6.6% of the slide); a glossy blue round creature wearing dark sunglasses (600 by 600 px); the four-colour Slack hash logo on a soft white disc (600 by 600 px); the Tableau logo of multicoloured bars in a white oval (983 by 983 px); and a violet-blue lightning-bolt glyph with a lighter inner facet (220 by 220 px). A blue user silhouette with a dark checkmark in its lower right sits at the left of the bottom bar, overlapping the bar edge (600 by 600 px). Four yellow glossy four-point sparkle stars are placed at the left edge near the top of the cards, the left edge below them, the lower right corner overlapping the card row, and at the far right edge; each is a single gold star with soft highlights. The Salesforce cloud logo is a light blue cloud with white lowercase wordmark salesforce, top right (1960 by 1372 px). No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid light blue #CFE9FE covering the whole slide. Flat, bright pastel style on a light sky-blue ground with white cards. Card outer frames are translucent white rounded panels (about 59% opacity) with inner white cards that have small rounded corners and no visible shadow. Title is set in a bold geometric sans-serif (AvantGarde Demi style) at about 5.9% of slide height, in navy #002775 for 'Built-In value to power your' and royal blue #023AC0 for 'Agentic Transformation'. Card body text is the same family in navy #002775 at about 3.0% of slide height, centred, with tight line spacing. The bottom message is one line of navy #002775 text in the same family. Shapes have no outlines and no gradients; the sparkles and icons carry soft 3D shading.

**Layout**

Three equal columns of pricing cards sit side by side below a left-aligned two-line title that runs across the top-left, with the salesforce logo at top right. Each column has a coloured top tab, a light card with a centred two-line heading, and three rows of tier label plus price box; a full-width white rounded bar with seven icons spans the bottom margin. Margins are about 7% left and right and the columns are evenly spaced; there is no footer, slide number or date.

**Visuals**

Three pricing cards, each a rounded light-blue panel with a coloured top tab (teal, magenta, navy) and a small gold coin icon with a four-point star at its top right. Each card has three rows; each row is a dark blue tier label box joined to a white rounded price box. Card 1 (Financial Services Cloud Sales): FSC Sales Core $325 per user/per month; FSC Sales Advanced $500 per user/per month; FSC Sales Max $700 per user/per month. Card 2 (Financial Services Cloud Service): FSC Service Core $325 per user/per month; FSC Service Advanced $500 per user/per month; FSC Service Max $700 per user/per month. Card 3 (Financial Services Cloud Sales & Service): FSC Sales & Service Core $350 per user/per month; FSC Sales & Service Advanced $525 per user/per month; FSC Sales & Service Max $725 per user/per month. No chart axes or legend. Bottom white rounded bar holds seven small 3D-style app icons in this order from left: teal square with arrow, pink heart, multicoloured ring of dots, blue person with checkmark, multicoloured hash-style Slack-like mark, blue smiling face, purple lightning bolt. At bottom left a green spreadsheet/calculator-style 3D icon with yellow sparkle stars overlaps the left edge of the first card; a small yellow star sits beside it. Icons are flat-lit with soft shadows; no photographs. Logos: salesforce cloud logo, top right.

**Design**

Background: Solid pale blue #CFE9FE fill across the whole slide. Pale sky-blue background with flat cards; cards have soft drop shadows and rounded corners of about 2% of slide width. Title in AvantGardeDemiSFDC navy #002775, bold weight, about 5.9% of slide height per line, set in two lines. Card headings in the same navy #002775 at about 3.5% of slide height. Tier label boxes are solid blue #022AC0 with white demi-bold text about 2.2% of slide height. Price text is navy #002775 at about 3.7%; per-user text is navy #002775 at about 2.0%. Card tabs: teal #0B827C, magenta #B60554, navy #002775. Card fill #E1F2FF with a 1 px navy #002775 outline on price boxes and card edges. Price boxes are white with a thin blue outline and rounded right corners. Bottom bar white with a navy-blue soft shadow.

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Breaks each bundle into priced line items and compares the total standalone value to the bundle price, making the value argument concrete.
- Review: The consequential weaknesses are legibility and density: slide 11 has a tiny itemised price list inside the bars, slides 38 and 41 to 44 rely on unreadable screenshots with thin labels, and slides 25, 27 and 37 carry very small text and callouts.
- Worth keeping: Keep the editions slide 11 structure of paired bars with a value/price multiple, the problem framing on slide 20 with its single stat, and the clear title-plus-subtitle-plus-benefit-list pattern of slide 35.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or crop the screenshots on slides 38 and 41 to 44 to the one relevant region and add a one-line takeaway per slide, and raise the minimum text size on slides 11, 12, 25 and 27.

**Text**

New FSC bundling delivers significantly more value / vs earlier editions / Financial Services Cloud Sales & / Service Core Edition / Financial Services Cloud Sales & / Service Advanced Edition / Financial Services Cloud Sales & / Service Max Edition / $1,300+ / $900+ / $575+ / Value vs A1E / $500+ / $725 / $350+ / Value vs UE / $525 / $150+ / Value vs EE / $350 / FSC Sales & / Service Core / FSC Sales & / Service Core / FSC Sales & / Service Advanced / FSC Sales & / Service Advanced / FSC Sales & / Service Max / FSC Sales & / Service Max / Core / per user/per month / Standalone / Value / Advanced / per user/per month / Standalone / Value / Max / per user/per month / Standalone / Value / 1.5x / value / price / 1.7x / value / price / 1.9x / value / price / Coworker $50 / Headless $50 / Maps Advanced $125 / AF Workforce Mgmt. $50 / Salesforce Contracts $50 / Data Detect $152 (15%) / Security Center $101 / (10%) / Archive (10GB) $1 / Backup & Recovery (10GB) / $1 / Sales Enablement $100 / Tableau Next Standard $40 / Slack Business+ $15 / Data Detect $105 (15%) / Security Center $70 (10%) / Archive (10GB) $1 / Backup & Recovery (10GB) / $1 / Flex Credits $25 / Flex Credits $2.50 / Tableau Next Standard $40 / Slack Business+ $15 / Premier Success Plan $122 / (30%)

**Layout**

Three equal columns sit side by side on a light blue field, each in a rounded white-tinted panel with a bold two-line headline across the top spanning the full width at left, and the Salesforce cloud logo at the top right. Each column has a navy pill header at its top, a bar chart in its middle with a navy baseline, axis labels and a per-user caption beneath, and a white rounded value-to-price box at the bottom; margins are about 3 percent on the sides, and there is no footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Three paired bar charts with no axis scales printed, drawn as solid navy bars on a navy baseline. Column 1 (Financial Services Cloud Sales & Service Core Edition): bar 'FSC Sales & Service Core' labelled $350 per user/per month with category label 'Core', and a second bar 'FSC Sales & Service Core' stacked with a white dotted-outline box labelled $500+ (standalone value) listing Flex Credits $2.50, Tableau Next Standard $40, Slack Business+ $15, Premier Success Plan $122 (30%); a dotted teal leader line runs from the $350 bar top to the standalone top with a double-headed grey arrow labelled $150+ Value vs EE. Column 2 (Advanced): bar 'FSC Sales & Service Advanced' labelled $525, and a second bar 'FSC Sales & Service Advanced' with a white dotted box labelled $900+ listing Sales Enablement $100 through Flex Credits $25; a double-headed grey arrow labelled $350+ Value vs UE joins the two bar tops by a dotted teal leader. Column 3 (Max): bar 'FSC Sales & Service Max' labelled $725, and a second bar 'FSC Sales & Service Max' with a white dotted box labelled $1,300+ listing Coworker $50 through Backup & Recovery (10GB) $1; a double-headed grey arrow labelled $575+ Value vs A1E joins the bar tops with a dotted teal leader. Each bar pair carries a small yellow plus sign on the standalone bar top. Bar heights are relative, not scaled: the Core standalone bar is about 2.3 times the Core bar height in the drawing, Advanced standalone about 1.5 times, Max standalone about 1.9 times. Value ratios printed in the white boxes: 1.5x, 1.7x and 1.9x, each with 'value / price' beneath. Decorative four-point yellow stars: one large near the left edge of column 1 at about x 2.3 y 41.1, one small at x 1.7 y 47.4, one at x 64.7 y 83.0 between columns 2 and 3, and a small one beside it. The Salesforce cloud logo sits top right in blue.

**Design**

Background light sky blue #CFE9FE. Headline navy #002775 for 'New FSC bundling delivers' and cobalt blue #023AC0 for 'significantly more value vs earlier editions'. Panels are white at 40 percent opacity over the blue field with rounded corners of about 2 percent of slide width and no visible border. Pills are cobalt #023AC0 with fully rounded ends and a soft grey drop shadow; pill labels white with yellow #F3BD29 highlight words Core, Advanced and Max. Bars solid #023AC0 with no outline. Value box text cobalt #023AC0 with 'value / price' in dark navy #002775. Typefaces: AvantGardeDemiSFDC, a geometric sans-serif demi-bold, for headline, pills, prices and bar labels; SalesforceSans-Regular and SalesforceSans-Bold, humanist sans-serif, for bar captions, detail lists and value/price lines. Headline cap height about 5.9 percent of slide height; pill text about 2.5 percent; price labels about 3.2 percent; detail list lines about 1.1 percent. Value boxes have a soft shadow and rounded corners.

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Enlarge or crop the screenshots on slides 38 and 41 to 44 to the one relevant region and add a one-line takeaway per slide, and raise the minimum text size on slides 11, 12, 25 and 27.

**Text**

Evaluate your footprint & plan your upgrade To amplify ROI
Collaboration & Insights
Give teams a seamless collaboration interface & conversational analytics with Slackbot & Tableau
Slack w/ Slackbot | Tableau Next
CORE
ADVANCED
MAX
Personalized Guidance
Keep your business growing with faster support, expert guidance, and fewer disruptions
Premier Success Plan
AI for Reps & Customers
Give teams instant insights and your customers instant responses with Sales AI, Help Agent & CSI
Casey (Help Agent), CSI, Sales AI
Trusted Security
Protect customer data, pass security audits, and work complex regulatory workflows with native trust, security, and compliance.
Backup and Recover, Archive, Data Detect, Security Center
Unmetered AI for Reps
Scale AI across customers and reps with complete catalog of financial services Agent templates, plus unmetered employee AI incl. Coworker
Agentforce for Financial Services Cloud | Unmetered Agentforce Coworker
Full Financial Services CRM Stack
Eliminate point solutions, giving customers one unified platform, purpose-built for financial services.
Workforce & Quality Management | SPM Suite | Ind. Add-ons
*Unmetered AI for Reps refers to all sales/service/industry AI agents bundled within A4FSC SKUs

**Layout**

Six equal-width rounded columns sit side by side across the lower two-thirds of the slide, each headed by a centred two-line or three-line title, with a stacked row of centred illustration, description text and a white pill label beneath. Three full-width tier bars (CORE, ADVANCED, MAX) overlay the lower parts of the columns, stepping in width and darkening downward. The title sits top-left in two lines, left-aligned at about 4.7% from the left edge, with the Salesforce cloud logo at top right; the footnote is right-aligned along the bottom edge. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Six flat illustrations, one per column, all boxed (not cropped, no overlay): (1) Collaboration & Insights: a small purple-framed dashboard screenshot showing a '2.4M' metric, a line chart and a blue popup button, lit evenly, dominant purple and white. (2) Personalized Guidance: a blue-bordered browser window titled 'My Premier Success Plan' with text lines and a small photo of people, dominant blue and white. (3) AI for Reps & Customers: a light UI screenshot of Sales AI and Account Research panels with a small circular gauge, dominant white and light blue. (4) Trusted Security: a 3D-style cyan shield with a padlock over a cloud and a document card on a blue square, dominant blue and cyan. (5) Unmetered AI for Reps: a glossy blue 3D robot-like head with dark visor, dominant cobalt blue. (6) Full Financial Services CRM Stack: a green 3D calculator/keypad icon with a gear, dominant green. Tier bars are flat solid shapes, not illustrations; there are no charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid light blue #CFE9FE. Light sky-blue slide background #CFE9FE with pale columns #EAF5FE. Title text in dark navy #002775 for 'Evaluate your footprint &' and royal blue #023AC0 for 'plan your upgrade To amplify ROI' and 'amplify ROI'. Headings are set in a bold geometric sans (AvantGarde Demi style, weight demi-bold) in navy #002775, roughly 3.2% of slide height for column headings and 5.9% for the title. Body text is a regular humanist sans (Salesforce Sans Regular) in navy #002775 at about 2.35% of slide height; pill labels are about 1.5%; footnote about 1.7%. Tier bars: CORE fill #1AB9FF with navy text #002775; ADVANCED fill #022AC0 with white text; MAX fill #002775 with white text, all heavy weight at about 2.5% height, uppercase, fully rounded pill ends. White pills (#FFFFFF) with navy text sit beneath the descriptions, fully rounded. Column panels have softly rounded corners (about 1% of width), no visible shadows, no gradients, no outlines.

### Slide 13

**Text**

Resources: Explore The Latest Pricing Editions / Financial Services Cloud Pricing Webpage / Financial Services Cloud Pricing Datasheet / Financial Services Cloud Add-on Pricing Webpage / Blog: Financial Institutions Need a Trusted Partner

**Layout**

Four equal white cards sit in a row across the middle-lower slide, evenly spaced with equal margins, spanning roughly 4% to 92% of the width and 26% to 83% of the height. The title sits top-left on one line at about 9% from the top, with the Salesforce cloud logo top-right at the same band. Each card holds a pale-blue rounded panel with a centred icon in its upper half and a left-aligned underlined link in its lower half. No footer, page number or source line is visible.

**Visuals**

Four flat, blue-toned icons, each about 110 by 110 pixels, centred in the pale-blue panels of the cards. Icon 1: a globe in mid and dark blue with lighter blue meridian and latitude lines, three dark navy dots and one white dot. Icon 2: a document pair, a dark navy sheet behind and a bright sky-blue front sheet with three horizontal blue lines. Icon 3: a bright blue circle with a dark navy plus sign, a white curved highlight at top-right and four small white dots on the rim. Icon 4: a browser window with a dark navy title bar holding three white dots and a small white flag, and a bright blue body with three horizontal blue lines. Decoration: four small yellow four-point sparkle stars with thin gold outlines, two on the left edge near the first card and two at the bottom-right near the fourth card. Logo: the Salesforce cloud logo in light blue with a white lowercase salesforce wordmark, top-right.

**Design**

Background: Solid pale blue #CFE9FE. Flat, clean style with no gradients. Background is solid pale blue. Title is set in a bold geometric sans-serif (AvantGardeDemiSFDC, close to ITC Avant Garde Demi), about 5.9% of slide height. The word Resources: is in royal blue #023AC0 and the remainder in dark navy #002775. Card link text is in royal blue #023AC0 with underlines, a demi-weight sans-serif at about 2.8% of slide height. Cards are white #FFFEFE with rounded corners of roughly 2% of slide width and no visible border or shadow. Inner panels are pale blue #CFE9FE with rounded corners of roughly 3% of slide width. Accent colour is yellow #F9C90D for sparkles.

### Slide 14

**Text**

Winter '27 / Innovations: / Banking / salesforce

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a tall white rounded panel with a concave right edge sits on the left, spanning about 5% to 41% of the width and 9% to 91% of the height, holding a 3D illustration. The three-line title is left-aligned in the open blue area to the right, starting at about 44% of the width and vertically centred around 40% to 69% of the height. The Salesforce cloud logo sits top right at about 90% x, 8% y. There is no footer, no slide number and no date.

**Visuals**

Illustration (left, inside white panel): a fuzzy bright-blue round character with a small ear-like bump at top left, wearing black glossy sunglasses with a white highlight, positioned at about x 7.7%, y 28% to 55%. Behind it is a rounded-square blue card (#0250D9) with a lighter blue diagonal up-right arrow icon near its top right corner. To its lower right is a light-blue rounded square (#00B3FF) with a brown-skinned 3D clapping-hands emoji overlapping its top-left edge. A translucent white pill (white at 40% opacity) with a white inner bar and a white circle holding a purple four-point sparkle sits across the two squares. Below the card is a horizontal progress bar: a rounded light-blue track (#00B3FF) with a darker blue fill (#0250D9) covering about the left 55%. Decorative 3D sparkles: a small yellow four-point star above the character, a larger light-blue four-point star above that, a small light-blue sparkle at the lower left of the card, and a small yellow sparkle beside the clapping hands. A large glossy yellow 3D star is cropped at the left edge of the slide, around y 63% to 79%, overlapping the white panel boundary. Lighting is soft and studio-like from above; the 3D renders have glossy highlights and soft drop shadows beneath them. Dominant colours: royal blue, light cyan blue, white, yellow and brown. No photograph; the illustration is boxed within the white panel and not cropped apart from the yellow star at the slide's left edge.

**Design**

Background is a vivid royal-blue gradient, brightest at the top (around #0460E8) and deepening to navy (around #0A2AA8) toward the right and lower right, with a cyan glow (around #00A6FF) along the bottom edge. The white panel is #FFFFFF with rounded outer corners (radius about 2% of width) on the left and right, and a concave curved right edge. Title text is white (#FFFFFF), set in a geometric grotesque sans-serif, AvantGardeDemiSFDC (demi-bold, mixed case), about 8.9% of slide height per line with tight leading (about 9.6% line pitch). The Salesforce logo is light blue (#56B4F8) with white lettering. Accent colours: #0250D9, #00B3FF, yellow (#F5C518) on the star and sparkles, purple (#6A1B9A) in the sparkle icon. Shapes have no outlines; the card and bars have rounded corners of about 8 to 10% of their height, with soft shadows under the card and clapping hands.

**Layout**

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a full-bleed pale blue field: a left text column (x 4.7% to about 45%) holds the title, subtitle, a short divider rule and three stacked headed sections, while the right half holds a horizontal rounded panel with a circular photo centred on it. Title sits top-left at about 8.6% from the top with a 4.7% left margin, subtitle directly beneath it, and the Salesforce cloud logo sits top-right near 90.5% x and 8.2% y; no footer or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Photograph: a smiling young Black woman with natural curly hair, wearing a mustard-yellow button-up shirt, seated and typing on a keyboard at the lower left of the frame, looking to the left; she is cropped by a circular mask, lit softly from the front, against a saturated royal-blue to deep-navy gradient backdrop; the circle sits on top of a faint white halo ring at 50% opacity. Illustration: a white and dark-visor cartoon robot mascot with blue accents, one arm raised in a wave and legs bent as if floating, placed over the upper-left of the circle; two small yellow four-point sparkle stars sit at the lower left of the circle. Diagram: a horizontal navy line with an arrowhead on its right end runs from about 54% to 89% x at about 79% y, passing behind a solid bright-blue pill labelled 'Transfers client context at escalation'. Two translucent rounded panels (white at 85% opacity) form one wide card behind the circle, with two text labels on either side. Logo: Salesforce cloud logo, light blue cloud with white lowercase 'salesforce' wordmark, top right.

**Design**

Background pale sky blue #CFE9FE, flat with no gradient in the text area. Title colour royal blue #022AC0; subtitle and body text navy #001E5B; panel labels and descriptions dark navy #032D60; section headings royal blue #022AC0; accent pill bright blue #066AFE with white text; divider rule light blue #8FD0F8. Title typeface is AvantGarde Demi (geometric sans, bold weight) at about 6.7% of slide height; subtitle is Salesforce Sans regular at about 4.4%; section headings are Salesforce Sans bold at about 3.7%; body is Salesforce Sans regular at about 3.2%; panel labels are AvantGarde Demi at about 2.7% and panel descriptions Salesforce Sans regular at about 1.9%. Divider is a thin line about 0.3% of height in light blue. The white card has rounded corners of roughly 3.5% of slide height with a soft drop shadow; the pill has fully rounded ends and a soft blue shadow beneath it; the circle photo has a soft white halo ring. No strokes on text.

### Slide 18

**Text**

Origination Checklist / Streamline loan decisioning with a unified, auditable Origination Checklist for / financial review / Unified Financial Review / See all applicant income, expenses, and / liabilities across documents and records / in one connected view, no spreadsheet / toggling / Instant Eligibility Insights / Automatically calculate key ratios and / determine loan eligibility the moment / applicant data changes / Configurable, Auditable / Decisioning / Admins configure calculation logic once, / every update and resulting decision stays / traceable for compliance

**Layout**

Single-column text block on the left from about 4% to 35% of slide width, with the title at top left (y 7.7%) and the subtitle directly beneath it spanning about 80% of width. Three feature blocks (bold heading then regular body) stack down the left column from y 30% to y 87%. A large product screenshot card is placed on the right, bleeding off the right edge of the slide, from about x 42% to the edge and y 23% to 98%; there is no footer, page number or slide number.

**Visuals**

Illustration and screenshot. (1) A large white rounded-corner card with a soft grey drop shadow sits behind the product screenshot, spanning about x 43.8% to the right edge and y 25.1% to 100%, cropped by the right slide edge. (2) A screenshot of the Salesforce Affordability and Serviceability Calculator UI, placed at about x 41.8%, y 22.8%, cropped at the right edge. The top shows a horizontal tab bar with tabs Eligibility Analysis, Affordability (selected, blue text with blue underline), Tradelines, Collateral, C360, Document Centre and CAM Report. Below is the page header 'Affordability and Serviceability Calculator' with a blue-and-white calculator icon at left and outlined pill buttons '+ Add Month', 'Export' and 'Save' at right (Save cut off). Next is an 'Agentforce Summary' panel with timestamp 'Today at 10:37 AM', a paragraph of summary text with bolded '$520,645.00' and '7.00%.', a solid blue pill button 'Simulate Scenarios', thumbs up and thumbs down icons at right, and a 'Sources (2)' list with 'Bank Statement 2025 • Document' (grey document icon) and 'Plaid Integration • External Link' (red globe icon). Table at bottom: columns Line Item | % Consideration | Actuals ($) with sub-columns Feb 25, Mar 25, Apr 25 | Projected ($) with sub-columns May 25, June 25, July 25 (cut off at right edge). Rows: Basic Pay | 100 | 5000 | 5000 | 5000 | 5200 | 5200 | 5200; Fixed Allowances | 100 | 500 | 500 | 500 | 500 | 500 | 500; Variable Allowances | 100 | 100 | 200 | 100 | 150 | 150 | 150; Expenses & Deduction | 100 | 800 | 800 | 800 | 850 | 850 | 850. The Projected columns have a light blue tint. (3) Four glossy light-blue 3D sparkle/star icons: one at about x 92.8%, y 22.8% (small), one at x 90.6%, y 19.3% (small), one at x 40.6%, y 75.8% overlapping the card's lower-left corner, all in sky blue. (4) A light-blue 3D Salesforce cloud logo with white lowercase 'salesforce' wordmark at top right, x 90.5%, y 8.2%.

**Design**

Background: Solid pale sky blue #CFE9FE covering the entire slide. Flat pale sky-blue background #CFE9FE with no gradient or pattern. Title in bold geometric display face (AvantGarde Demi style) in cobalt blue #022AC0. Subtitle in regular humanist sans (SalesforceSans-Regular) in dark navy #001E5B. Feature headings in SalesforceSans-Bold in strong blue #062ABF; body text in SalesforceSans-Regular in dark blue #023779, line spacing about 1.2. Title cap height about 6.7% of slide height; subtitle about 3.95%; headings about 3.2%; body about 2.8%. Screenshot card has rounded corners (about 3% of slide height radius), white fill and soft grey shadow; UI uses blue #0176D3 accents and grey #706E6B text. Sparkles are glossy sky blue #5BB8F0 with white highlights.

### Slide 19

**Text**

Winter '27 / Innovations: / Wealth

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue gradient background with a large white rounded panel on the left third holding illustrations; the title is left-aligned in three lines at the vertical centre to the right of the panel. The Salesforce logo sits in the top-right corner. No footer or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Illustration cluster on the white panel: a fuzzy blue 3D character head with black sunglasses at upper left, a blue rounded square with a white diagonal up-right arrow icon, a light-blue rounded square with a 3D brown clapping hands emoji overlapping it, a translucent white pill with a purple sparkle icon and a white bar, a light-blue horizontal progress bar with a darker blue filled segment about 45% of its length at lower left, small yellow and light-blue four-point sparkles above, and a glossy yellow 3D star cut off at the left edge of the panel. The Salesforce cloud logo in white lettering on light blue at top right. Composition is flat front-on with the illustration cluster in the panel's left half.

**Design**

Background is a saturated royal-blue gradient, brighter blue #0060F0 at top fading to deeper #0230B0 at right and a lighter cyan glow #00A3FF along the bottom edge. Title text is white #FFFFFF in a geometric sans-serif (Avant Garde Demi style) at medium-bold weight, about 8.9% of slide height per line with tight leading. The white panel has rounded corners of about 2% of slide width, with its right edge curved inward as a concave arc. Illustration accents use blues #0250D9, #00B3FF, yellow #F5C518 and a purple sparkle #6A1B9A. No shadows on the title; soft drop shadows under the 3D illustrations.

### Slide 20

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Frames the wealth problem with a short set of pain points and a single question, setting up the product without naming it yet.
- Worth keeping: Keep the editions slide 11 structure of paired bars with a value/price multiple, the problem framing on slide 20 with its single stat, and the clear title-plus-subtitle-plus-benefit-list pattern of slide 35.

**Text**

The next-gen wealth dilemma
Expectations are compounding faster than advisor capacity.
Strained advisor capacity
Too much time spent managing work instead of relationships.
Rising expectations
Clients expect proactive, personalized engagement at every stage.
Fragmented execution
Data, systems and workflows are disconnected across the lifecycle.
How do we redesign the advisor experience so they spend more time growing relationships and less time managing the work around them?
67%
of advisor time is spent on admin, not advice

**Layout**

Full-bleed landscape background. Kicker and headline are left-aligned at the top, starting at about x 5.4% and y 13.8%, and span most of the width. Three stacked white cards in the left column (x 5.9% to 55.9%, y 33% to 76.5%) with equal gaps. A large solid blue panel sits in the right column (x 59.6% to 94.4%, y 31.6% to 84.6%) inside a pale translucent frame. A circular badge overlaps the lower-right corner of the blue panel. No footer, source line or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

1) Full-bleed illustration of a pale sky with rolling green grassy hills, pine trees at the left and right edges, a pair of small pines on the horizon at about x 28% to 32%, y 70% to 80%, and a glowing lavender-white stepping path winding from the bottom edge toward the horizon. Soft daylight, no overlay. 2) Three small icons on pale blue tiles, one per card: a glossy blue plus-in-circle (strained advisor capacity), a blue envelope-style image with a pin (rising expectations), and a blue 3D cube with motion lines and a check badge (fragmented execution). 3) Progress ring in the lower-right circular badge: a light blue track with a bright blue arc running clockwise from 12 o'clock through about two-thirds of the circle, with the figure 67% and the caption inside. No axes or legend.

**Design**

Background light sky blue, about #CDE9FF at the top, fading into grass greens below. Kicker text bright blue #022AC0. Headline and card titles dark navy #001E5B. Card body text dark navy #001E5B. Blue panel fill #022AC0 with white question text #FFFFFF and light blue highlight #90D0FE. Figure 67% in navy #002775. Typefaces: AvantGardeDemiSFDC, a geometric demi-bold sans-serif, for headings and the question; SalesforceSans-Regular, a humanist sans-serif, for card body copy. Headline size about 5.2% of slide height; kicker about 3.95%; card titles about 3.2%; card body about 2.5%; question about 4.2%. Cards are white with pale blue borders and soft blue shadows, corner radius small, about 1% of slide height. Badge is off-white at 92% opacity with a faint shadow.

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a light blue field: a left text column starting at about 4.7% from the left edge and a right visual zone holding a photo of a woman with the Astro character and floating UI cards. The headline sits top-left at about 13.7% from the top, with three bullets stacked below it, each with a leading icon. The stat card sits bottom-left at about 86.5% from the top, and the Salesforce logo sits top-right. There is no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Photograph: a smiling woman with shoulder-length dark hair, in a light grey-blue blazer, looking down toward a dark teal laptop. She is lit softly from the front, and the image is cut out with no background, so she blends into the light blue field. Illustration: the Astro character, a 3D brown-haired mascot in a brown hooded top with 'AI+ CRM' printed on the chest, holding a glowing blue orb in one hand and reaching out with the other. Three floating UI cards with white pill shapes and soft shadows: (1) 'Idle Cash Detected' with a yellow 'Attention Needed' tag, a light-blue 'Draft Outreach' tag and a blue cursor arrow; (2) a bright cyan #00B3FF pill reading 'Reach out to at-risk clients' with a small circular woman headshot on its left; (3) a white card reading 'Upcoming Client Meeting' with a blue round avatar of a cartoon face in sunglasses and a teal 'Prep Brief Ready' tag. Three blue sparkle shapes with glossy gradients sit on the right edge. Icons: a navy-to-cyan paper-plane arrow beside bullet 1, a blue square sparkle icon beside bullet 2, and a blue stacked-lines icon beside bullet 3. Stat: '+50%' in navy and blue type on a white card.

**Design**

Background light sky blue #CFE9FE, flat with no gradient. Headline in heavy geometric sans, navy #001E5B, with the phrase 'they need one that does the work for them' in royal blue #022AC0. Bullet text in regular humanist sans, navy #001E5B. Accents in royal blue #022AC0 and cyan #00B3FF. Headline is about 5.2% of slide height and body about 4.4%. Cards are white pills with rounded ends, thin white outer halo at 25% opacity, and soft blue-grey drop shadows. The stat card is a white rectangle with small corner radius.

**Layout**

Two columns on a light blue slide. The left column (about x 4.7% to 40%) stacks the GA Now pill, the title Agentic Advisor, a two-line subtitle, four plain text lines, and a framed illustration at the bottom left. The right column (x 40.6% to 97.4%) is a 2 by 4 grid of equal rounded cards, each 27.9% wide and 19.1% high, with 2.2% horizontal and 2.5% vertical gutters; the cards run from y 11.3% to 95.2%. There is no separate page title above the grid, no footer, and no slide number. Left margin is about 4.7% and right margin about 2.6%; text is left-aligned throughout.

**Visuals**

Left bottom: a framed product illustration. A white 25% opacity frame (x 4.2%, y 62.3%, 33.8% by 32.7%) holds a solid #022AC0 royal blue panel (x 5.1%, y 63.5%, 32.1% by 30.3%) with a soft drop shadow. Inside the panel is a screenshot of the Agentic Advisor app, a Salesforce-style web window (about x 10.5%, y 67.8%, 19.5% by 21.9%) showing a top bar with search field and icons, a left app menu, a greeting 'Good evening, Ryan' with a short paragraph, card tiles titled Portfolio Review, Refinement Planning, Estate Planning Review, Insights, Top Tier Contact Gap, Upcoming Client Birthdays and Groups, plus small blue and red status pills. Overlapping the screenshot are: an Agentforce Actions panel at the upper left (x 7.6%, y 70.2%, 6.4% by 8.3%) listing Generate Email Draft, Add Task To Update Portfolio, Create Referral and Create Life Event, each with a small button; a Meeting Prep Brief panel at the lower right (x 27.0%, y 77.2%, 6.0% by 12.2%) with white fill, a Relationship Snapshot and Portfolio Overview sections; a glossy blue 3D orb (x 28.3%, y 69.9%, 3.2% by 5.6%); and a green 3D cube (x 9.1%, y 79.9%, 3.4% by 6.1%). Small sparkle icons in blue (x 27.2%, y 24.7%, 1.9% by 3.4%) sit beside the subtitle. Right grid: eight cards, each with a coloured outline and a badge (GA, Coming Soon) described under elements. Lighting is flat UI lighting; no photograph.

**Design**

Background is a flat pale sky blue #CFE9FE. Cards are white at 25% opacity over the blue background, giving a pale blue fill of about #DCF1FE, with 2 to 3 px outlines in the accent colour and rounded corners of about 1.5% of slide width. Main title and subtitle are royal blue #022AC0 in a bold geometric sans (Avant Garde Demi style, SFDC). Plain body lines in the left column are dark navy #002775 in Salesforce Sans Regular. Card labels (NEW FEATURE etc.) are dark navy #002674 in uppercase Avant Garde Demi, about 1.7% of slide height. Card titles are bold geometric sans in the accent colour, about 3.0% of slide height. Bullets and bullet text are Salesforce Sans Regular, about 2.0% of slide height, in #002674. Title text is about 7.4% of slide height; subtitle about 3.7%; body lines about 3.2%. Accent colours: Meeting Concierge #00B3FF; Run My Day #04E1CB; Enhanced Client Details #FF538A; Intelligent Referral Management #D17DFE; Prebuilt Data Connectors #F6BC06; Financial Advisor Agent #066AFE; Anywhere Advisor #8260F2; Book of Business Insights #45C65A. GA badges are solid navy #022AC0 with white bold text, except the Meeting Concierge badge which is a lighter blue #4A5FCF. Coming Soon pills use gradient fills: lavender #B9A0F5 to #9A7AF0 on Anywhere Advisor, and green #2ED47A to #1FB8A0 on Book of Business Insights. Shadows are soft and light on the GA Now pill and framed panel.

The screenshot shows a Miyazaki Annual Review meeting page with left column Client Summary card, Documents and Attendees collapsed cards, centre column Meeting Prep Tasks table with five rows (Confirm Schedu... 02/25/2026 Complete; Update Latest T... 03/12/2026 Complete; Reassess Risk 03/12/2026 In Progress; Update Profile 03/12/2026 Complete; Confirm financia... 03/12/2026 Pending; all owned by Shelly Ta...) and Meeting Prep Brief card, and right column Slack panel and Notes panel. Status pills are green (Complete), blue (In Progress), grey (Pending). Lighting is flat, even UI light; dominant colours white, light grey #F3F3F3 and Salesforce blue #0176D3. The screenshot is boxed with rounded corners and a soft shadow, not cropped. A blue 3D cartoon mascot character wearing sunglasses (about x 61.1%, y 11.4%, w 8.0%, h 14.1%) overlaps the top edge of the panel. A light blue Salesforce cloud logo sits at top right. Four-point sparkle stars in light blue #4DB8F0 decorate the panel edges at about (97.2, 23.5), (95.0, 20.1) and (51.1, 79.9). A blue segmented pill bar with four labelled segments: Pre Meeting (#00B3FF), In Meeting (#036CFD), Post Meeting (#022AC0), Meeting Mgmt (#001E5B), darkening left to right, each segment with rounded ends and a soft shadow.

**Design**

Background light sky blue #CFE9FE with a white rounded panel at about 74% opacity behind the screenshot. Title Meeting Concierge in a geometric sans (AvantGarde Demi style) bold, colour royal blue #022AC0, large (about 6.4% of slide height cap height as sized). Subtitle Help advisors do their best work in AvantGarde Demi, navy #001E5B. Bullets in SalesforceSans Regular, dark blue #002775, about 3.5% of slide height. New Enhancements in AvantGarde Demi #002775 about 3.7% of height, with a blue #0B4FD8 horizontal rule to its right. Small list lines in SalesforceSans Regular #002775 about 2.5% of height. GA Now pill: dark blue #0A2FB8 pill with white GA and a lighter blue #7FD3FF inner capsule labelled Now. +more in AvantGarde Demi #022AC0. Segment labels white AvantGarde Demi about 2.5% of height. Generous whitespace, no gradients on the background, soft drop shadow under the pill bar.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a light blue field. The left column (x 3.9% to about 44%) holds the GA Now pill logo at top, the title 'Run My Day' with subtitle 'Prioritize what matters most' beneath it, a thin divider rule, then three stacked heading-and-body pairs ending near y 82%. The right column holds a large dashboard screenshot in a white rounded frame, starting at x about 49.5% and y about 28% and running off the right edge and bottom area, with a salesforce cloud logo at top right (x 90.5%, y 8.2%) and four colour-graded pill tabs (Grow, Retain, Service, Comply) overlapping the bottom edge of the screenshot at y 86.2% to 93.5%. Left margin is about 3.9%. No footer, page number or source line is visible.

**Visuals**

Screenshot of a Salesforce-style 'Run My Day' dashboard, placed as a large rounded-corner frame cropped by the right and bottom slide edges, on a white panel with opacity 0.74. Top section: a centred greeting 'Good evening, Ryan' with a small line of grey subtext above a 'Today's Meetings' card row showing three meeting cards (Annual Review, Portfolio Review, Retirement Planning) with blue-link organiser and related-record names and status badges (In Prep in pale blue, Past Due in pale red). Middle section: a three-column panel with a 'Groups' list (Grow 7 with a yellow alert icon, Retain 15 in a selected solid blue #0A6CFF row with white text, Service 404 with a red alert icon, Comply 8 with a red alert icon), an 'Insights' list of three items, and a 'Top Tier Contact Gap' list with three contact cards each carrying a yellow 'Attention Needed' badge. Lighting is flat UI lighting with soft white and grey tones. Dominant colours are white, pale grey, bright blue and yellow badges. The screenshot is cropped on the right and bottom. A 3D cartoon blue mascot with dark sunglasses sits at the top edge of the frame (about x 56.5%, y 21.2%, w 8%, h 14.1%), overlapping the frame edge. Three 3D blue sparkle shapes appear: one at about x 48.0%, y 75.0% (w 4.1%, h 7.3%) overlapping the left edge of the frame, and two at top right near x 90.0%, y 24.5% and x 92.2%, y 27.9%. The four pill tabs are a horizontal row of rounded capsules, each with a left-to-right colour step: Grow in #00B3FF (x 55.1%, w 10.8%), Retain in #036CFD (x 63.4%, w 11.6%), Service in #022AC0 (x 72.6%, w 11.6%), Comply in #001E5B (x 81.9%, w 12.9%); each capsule has a white label and a small logo or icon on its left. The pills overlap one another and the bottom edge of the frame.

**Design**

Background: Solid light sky blue #CFE9FE across the full slide. Light sky-blue background (#CFE9FE). Title and heading colour is a strong royal blue (#022AC0), subtitle and accents are deep navy (#002775). Body text is dark navy (#001E5B). Title and headings use a geometric sans-serif display face, demi-bold weight, in sentence case with no capitals treatment; the title is about 6.9% of slide height, subtitle about 4.4%, headings about 3.2%, body text about 2.5%. Body text is a humanist sans-serif regular weight. A thin pale-blue divider rule (#8ED0F8-like, about 0.3% of slide height thick) runs horizontally under the subtitle. The GA Now logo is a deep blue (#0230B0-like) rounded pill with a white 'GA' and a pale-blue inner pill containing white 'Now'. The screenshot frame is white with opacity 0.74 and rounded corners, with a soft drop shadow beneath the pill tabs. Pill tabs are fully rounded capsules with soft shadows.

Client Pulse
Generated at 9:37 AM
Prompt: Generate Client Pulse for Person Account
1. Summary:
Jennifer Blake is an active client with a multi-account relationship totaling $5,127,500 across five accounts, anchored by The Blake Family Trust. Her planning focus is retirement and college savings for two children, managed under the Blake Family Financial Plan.
2. What changed:
Three cases have been opened, including a high-priority Trust Document Amendment Request and a 529 Plan Contribution Increase request, both created on 8/26/2026. The address change case remains in working status. No last interaction date is on record, so elapsed time cannot be determined.
3. Why it matters:
The open high-priority trust amendment case warrants prompt attention given the size of The Blake Family Trust at $2,780,000. The 529 contribution increase request aligns with Olivia's college goal, where the current balance of $124,500 remains $75,500 short of the $200,000 target due July 2027.
4. Key Data Points:
Account Value - $5,127,500 across five active accounts (most recent balances as of 7/30/2026 and 9/29/2025)
Top Goal - Jennifer & Stephen's Retirement, IN_PROGRESS, $1,455,226 of $3,455,555 (42%), target January 8, 2037
Financial Plan - Retirement, in Progress
Relationship Status - Client
Open Cases - 3 open; Trust Document Amendment (High, in Progress), Primary Address Change (Medium, Working), 529 Plan Contribution Increase (Medium, New)

**Layout**

Centred header block at top: small blue subtitle above a large navy title, both centred on the slide's horizontal axis, with the Salesforce cloud logo at top right. Below, a central circular portrait framed by a thin cyan ring is surrounded by four product screenshots in the corners (Client Pulse top left, Next-Gen Household top right, Portfolio Management bottom left, Top Tier Contact Gap bottom right), each with a dark blue rounded caption pill overlapping its lower or inner edge. No visible footer or page number.

**Visuals**

Four UI screenshots and one portrait photograph, plus a Salesforce logo and a small blue orb icon. (1) Client Pulse: a white card screenshot with small grey-black text sections, a blue info icon and expand icon top right, boxed by a light translucent white frame with soft shadow, partly covered at its lower edge by the Client Pulse pill. (2) Next-Gen Household Experience: a tree diagram in a light grey panel. A rounded rectangle labelled Marcel Household with a blue icon connects by a thin grey elbow line to a white card Members (3) with a plus icon, containing a Marcel Green row with small avatar, purple star and Head of Household label; further connectors run to rounded rows Related Households (2) and Related Accounts (5), each with chevron and blue plus-in-circle icon. The panel is cut off at its bottom edge. (3) Recent AUM Trend: a white card with a line chart titled Recent AUM Trend, headline value $128.15K, y-axis labelled $127K to $128.2K in steps of about $0.2K, x-axis May and Jun, a single blue smoothed line from about $127.1K in May dipping slightly to about $127.0K in June then rising steeply to $128.15K at the right end, with blue point markers at the start, trough and end. Next to it, a Liquidity Monitor card with values $4.9K Actual and $0 Required, a solid bright blue horizontal bar spanning most of the width with a dark vertical end marker at its right, and the label Actual: $4.9K below. (4) Top Tier Contact Gap: a white card with blue heading, grey body paragraph, outlined Dismiss link in blue at lower left and an outlined pill button Draft Email with navy border at lower right, with a small blue glowing orb icon overlapping its bottom edge. (5) Portrait: a smiling woman with long dark hair and a tan jacket over a grey top, cropped as a circle, set on a flat lavender-blue #7F8CED disc, lit evenly from the front, framed by a thin cyan ring. (6) Salesforce logo: a bright blue cloud with white lowercase wordmark salesforce, top right.

**Design**

Background: Solid pale blue #CFE9FE filling the entire slide. Pale sky-blue background #CFE9FE with no gradient or pattern. Main title in heavy geometric sans (AvantGarde Demi style, closest common family Century Gothic or Avant Garde) in navy #001E5B, about 4.7% of slide height cap-height-scaled, sitting on one line. Subtitle in same family, medium weight, royal blue #022AC0, about 3.0% of slide height. Callout headings in the same family in bright cyan #00B3FF, about 2.2% of slide height. Callout body text in white regular weight, about 1.7% of slide height, centred. Accent colours: royal blue #022AC0 pills, lavender #7F8CED disc, cyan #00B3FF ring and headings. Pills are fully rounded pill shapes with no visible border. Screenshot frames are white rounded rectangles with soft grey drop shadows and translucent white 25% opacity backing panels offset behind them. Ring stroke is roughly 1% of slide width, cyan.

### Slide 28

_Long standard legal text on this slide is summarised, not transcribed; its exact words are in the measured list below._

**Text**

GA Now / salesforce / Prebuilt Financial Data Connector / Custodial data, connected in clicks / The full custodial picture, unified / Accounts, households, holdings, and AUM, / no manual exports, no third-party dashboards. / Ready for Data 360 / A prebuilt data kit deploys data bundles and maps / advisor codes for accurate attribution. / Live in minutes, not months / Easily connect, validate credentials, deploy data / streams, and track daily syncs ands status. / One connection, no more swiveling / The same data powers Financial Portfolio, Run My Day, / and every insight downstream. / Data Cloud / FSC / Meeting Center / Run my Day / Client 360 / Data Driven / Actionability / Desktop / FSC Data Kit / Data Streams / DLOs / DMOs / Mappings / Transforms / Calculated Insights / Financial Accounts / Holdings / Positions / Balances / Target Allocations / Deposits & Withdrawals / Portfolio Performance / AUM Trends / and more… / 20+ Streams & DMOs populated via the integration

**Layout**

Two columns on a light blue field: a left text column starting at about 4 percent from the left edge, and a large rounded blue-framed diagram panel on the right spanning about 42.5 to 99.5 percent of the width and 22.9 to 98.1 percent of the height. The GA Now pill sits top left at about y 7, the title sits directly below it at about y 14, the salesforce cloud logo sits top right at about x 90.5, and the subtitle and a thin rule sit under the title; all text is left aligned. There is no footer, no slide number, and no source line.

**Visuals**

Diagram (flow-style architecture chart) inside a rounded dark blue #022AC0 frame with a white inner panel. At the top centre, a Data Cloud cloud glyph in light blue #4DB3F5 with the words Data Cloud in small dark navy text sits above a square outlined gear icon in periwinkle #7FA8F5 with a light blue border; two elbow connector lines in light blue #8CCBF5 run from the gear down and split to the top of the central column. On the left, a tall rounded rectangle outlined in bright cyan #00B3FF (about 2 px stroke, corner radius about 1 percent of width, white fill) is labelled FSC above it, containing three stacked items: a storefront icon with the label Meeting Center, a browser-window-with-check icon with the label Run my Day, and another browser-window-with-check icon with the label Client 360, all in cyan #00B3FF; a dark navy #002775 rule sits under the box, with the caption Data Driven Actionability Desktop below it. A small gear icon in dark blue #022AC0 sits on a horizontal connector line between the FSC box and the central column, and a second identical gear sits on the connector between the central column and the right column. In the centre, seven pill-shaped bars in bright blue #066AFE, each about 17.8 percent of slide width and 4.3 percent of height, stacked vertically from top to bottom: FSC Data Kit, Data Streams, DLOs, DMOs, Mappings, Transforms, Calculated Insights; the bars are linked by vertical light blue #8CCBF5 connector lines between them and by a bracket line on the left (x about 56) and right (x about 75.7) that runs the full column height. On the right, eight pill-shaped bars in dark blue #022AC0 with white text, stacked vertically: Financial Accounts, Holdings, Positions, Balances, Target Allocations, Deposits and Withdrawals, Portfolio Performance, AUM Trends, and a ninth bar reading and more…; a light blue connector runs from the second gear to the Positions bar. At the bottom right of the panel, a wide full-width-of-column bar in dark navy #002775 with white text reads 20+ Streams and DMOs populated via the integration. The GA Now badge is a dark blue #022AC0 pill with a light blue inner pill, the salesforce logo is a light blue cloud with white lowercase wordmark, and a soft blue drop shadow surrounds the diagram panel. Nothing is cropped.

**Design**

Background: Solid light blue #CFE9FE, flat with no gradient, photograph or pattern. Flat light blue background #CFE9FE. Title in royal blue #022AC0, subtitle in dark navy #002775, section headings in royal blue #022AC0, body text in dark navy #001E5B, accent rule and connectors in light blue #7FCBF5 and #8CCBF5, bright blue accents #066AFE and #00B3FF, dark navy bar #002775. Typefaces: title, subtitle, headings, and diagram labels are AvantGardeDemiSFDC, a geometric demi-bold sans-serif; body paragraphs are SalesforceSans-Regular, a regular-weight humanist sans-serif. Title is about 5.7 percent of slide height, headings about 3.2 percent, subtitle about 3.5 percent, body about 2.5 percent, diagram labels about 1.6 percent. Panel has a royal blue rounded frame with corner radius about 2 percent of height, white inner panel with rounded corners, a soft blue drop shadow beneath, and no gradients. Pills have fully rounded ends with no outlines.

### Slide 29

**Text**

Deploy in Minutes. / Configure with Clicks. / A prebuilt suite with workflows, / guardrails, and integrations, / ready to activate and adapt to / your firm. / Out of the box agentic experience for advisors / Setup and customization with clicks, not code / Easily connects your existing systems / Built to scale, designed for control / Activate / salesforce / Partner Connector / Active / Client ID / Deploy Streams / Manage Streams / Financial Services / Agentforce Meeting Concierge / Configure agentic prompts and data amplification for client interactions. / Get Started / Enter Partner Credentials / Connector Name / BridgeIT / * Client ID / Test Connection / Connection Successful. / Permission Sets / Assign to profiles or permission set groups for broader access. / All Users / 10+ Items - Sorted by Full Name / Name / System Administrator / Wealth Management Advisor / Senior Wealth Advisor / Custom: Meeting Center Users / Activate This Playbook / Enable at runtime / Disabled / How would you like to create your playbook? / Create From Scratch / Build a new playbook from the ground up. Define stages, configure components, and customize every details. / Periodic Review / Ideal for quarterly or annual check-ins with existing clients. / Prospecting / Streamlines the onboarding process from first contact to relationship establishment. / Meeting Prep Readiness / Client Summary / Meeting Prep Tasks / Documents / Attendees / Meeting Prep Brief / Agentforce Meeting Concierge / Periodic Review / Prospecting / Cumulus Playbook

**Layout**

Two columns on a light blue slide: a left text column occupying roughly the left half with the title at top-left, a subtitle paragraph beneath, a thin horizontal rule, and four bold bullet-style lines at the bottom; a right column holds a large rounded-left light blue panel containing a collage of overlapping product screenshots and UI cards. Left margin about 4.7% of slide width, text starts at about 12% from top. No footer, page number or source line is visible.

**Visuals**

A collage of about ten product UI screenshots on a #90D0FE panel with a curved left edge, arranged loosely in a grid-like scatter, with two cursor arrow icons and a robot mascot 'Activate' pill. Top-left screenshot (about 54.4% x, 10.8% y, 19.4% w, 25.1% h): Agentforce Meeting Concierge web page with a Financial Services breadcrumb, title, paragraph, a video thumbnail with play button showing a cartoon mascot, a Suggested for You badge and list rows Periodic Review, Prospecting and Cumulus Playbook with status pills. Partner Connector card (76.2%, 18.5%, 19.1% x 12.9%): white card with a pink connector icon, Active green pill with check, Client ID field, outlined Deploy Streams and Manage Streams buttons in blue. Meeting Prep screenshot (59.9%, 38.6%, 15.1% x 26.5%): white app window with Client Summary, Meeting Prep Tasks, Documents, Attendees cards and a selected blue-outlined Meeting Prep Brief card. Concierge card (79.8%, 34.6%, 10.9% x 20.2%): white card with pale lavender top band showing a small screenshot, Financial Services label, title and Get Started blue button. Enter Partner Credentials form (76.6%, 57.8%, 14.8% x 11.7%): white card with input fields and Test Connection outlined button and green check Connection Successful. Permission Sets table (54.7%, 69.3%, 14.5% x 17.1%): white card with All Users list table of five rows and a toggle switch in grey labelled Disabled. Playbook chooser (70.2%, 73.3%, 25.3% x 19.6%): white card with four option tiles, Create From Scratch selected with blue outline and check, Periodic Review, Prospecting with teal and blue icons. Salesforce cloud logo in white-on-blue at top right (90.5%, 8.2%, 5.2% x 6.5%) in #1B96FF blue cloud. Floating pill with blue sparkle star icon at right edge (92.3%, 60.8%, 7.7% x 8.7%). Activate pill with robot mascot icon in dark blue sunglasses and a blue arrow cursor below. Cursor arrow near Clicks in lavender-blue. Lighting flat, no photographs. Nothing cropped except the cards bleeding off the panel edge at the right.

**Design**

Background: Solid light blue #CFE9FE with a deeper blue panel #90D0FE on the right half. Light sky-blue background #CFE9FE with a deeper #90D0FE panel on the right. Title first line 'Deploy in Minutes.' in dark navy #001E5B, second line 'Configure with Clicks.' in vivid royal blue #022AC0, both in a heavy geometric sans-serif (AvantGarde Demi style, bold, approx. 6.4% of slide height cap height zone). Subtitle in a regular-weight humanist sans-serif (Salesforce Sans Regular style) at 4.9% of slide height in navy #001E5B, line spacing about 5.3% of height. Thin rule in light blue #90D0FE across left column. Four bullets in bold AvantGarde Demi style at 3.2% of slide height in navy #001E5B, spaced about 7.2% apart, no bullet glyphs. Cards are white with thin light grey borders and soft shadows, corner radius about 1 to 2% of width. Floating Activate pill is white-to-pale-blue with soft shadow and rounded ends.

The Salesforce cloud logo sits top right at about 90% x, 8% y; no slide number or footer is visible.

**Visuals**

Product collage on the right: (1) a faint white translucent rounded panel behind the collage, about 41% to 100% x and 9% to 100% y; (2) a Salesforce Cumulus app window screenshot, about 52% to 98% x and 26% to 97% y, showing a navy top bar with a search field, a left app launcher, and the tabs 'Miyazaki Household' and 'Miyazaki Annual Review'. The main panel reads 'Miyazaki Annual Review' with status 'In Progress' and date '2026/02/18, 09:00 AM - 10:00 AM', with tabs 'Meeting Prep', 'Meeting Session' and 'Meeting Follow-Up'. A 'Meeting Prep Readiness' progress bar and a 'Refresh' button sit above a 'Client Summary' card (client Eriko Miyazaki, age 51, AUM $4.8M, Moderate Growth, with bullets on goals, financial context, sentiment and risk sensitivity), a 'Meeting Prep Tasks' table with columns Subject, Owner, Due Date and Status (rows: 'Confirm Schedu...' A right-hand Slack panel shows 'Better collaboration with Slack' with a message composer reading 'Post an update on this account or @mention someone to start the conversation'. A 'Notes' panel sits below it. (3) A white-bordered card 'Recent AUM Trend' with '$128.15K' and 'Most Recent AUM': a blue line chart, x axis May, Jun, Jul; y axis $127K to $128.2K in steps of about $0.2K; values about $127.9K in May, about $127.1K in Jun and about $128.1K in Jul, with the line rising steeply to Jul and dots at each month. (4) An 'Idle Cash Deployment (5)' tooltip card with an orange warning dot. Icons and illustrations: a glossy blue robot head with dark sunglasses at about 59% x and 22% y; a purple-grey 3D cube/gear shape at about 50% x and 56% y; a blue sparkle at about 94% x, 23% y; a larger blue sparkle at about 97% x, 31% y; two small blue sparkles beside the subtitle at about 27% x, 25% y. The collage is full-colour, bright and flat, with soft drop shadows under the cards and no photographic lighting.

**Design**

Background light sky blue #CFE9FE with no gradient.
