The GA Now badge is a rounded pill #022AC0 with 'GA' in white and 'Now' in white on a lighter pill #90D0FE, text about 2.5% of slide height. The COMING SOON bar is a fully rounded pill #022AC0 with 'COMING SOON' in white capitals about 2.7% of slide height on an inner rounded pill #90D0FE, and 'Available in' in white bold about 3.2% of slide height. ### Slide 31

**Text**

Winter '27 / Innovations: / Insurance

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue gradient background with a tall white rounded panel on the left side spanning roughly 5% to 41% of the width and 9% to 91% of the height; its right edge is a concave curve. Illustration cluster sits inside the panel's left portion. Title block is left-aligned in the right half of the slide, vertically centred around the middle, with three lines stacked. Salesforce cloud logo sits top-right. No footer or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Illustration cluster inside the white panel: a fuzzy blue 3D character head with black sunglasses at upper left; a large blue rounded square with a white diagonal up-right arrow icon; a small blue rounded square at lower right holding a 3D brown clapping hands emoji with yellow sparkles above; a white pill-shaped input bar with a purple four-point sparkle icon in a white circle at its left, overlapping the blue square; a blue progress bar at bottom with a darker blue filled portion about half its length; small blue and yellow four-point sparkle stars scattered; a large glossy yellow four-point star partly cropped at the left edge of the slide. Salesforce cloud logo in light blue #4DB8FF with white 'salesforce' lowercase wordmark at top right. Overall lighting is soft studio 3D rendering.

**Design**

Background: deep blue gradient from bright royal blue #0A5CF0 at top to deeper navy #0230A8 at right and a lighter cyan glow #00A3FF along the bottom edge. Title text white #FFFFFF. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif similar to Avant Garde Demi, medium-bold weight, title line height about 9% of slide height, letter size about 8.9% of slide height. White panel #FFFFFF with rounded corners about 2% of slide height radius, right edge a concave curve. No visible body text, shadows soft under the illustration objects.

### Slide 32

**Text**

Claims
transformation
can’t wait
any longer.
Insurers know what needs to change,
but most are still catching up.
AI in claims cut liability assessment time by
23 days and reduced customer complaints
by 65% – yet most insurers have yet to
scale it.
Source: McKinsey, The Future of AI for the Insurance Industry, 2025
salesforce

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a large 3D illustration bleeds off the left and bottom edges across roughly the left half, and a right-hand text column starts at about 48.6% of slide width. The headline sits top-left of the column at y 4.8%, left-aligned on a flush left edge, with the Salesforce cloud logo top-right at x 90.5%, y 8.2%. The pill-shaped call-out, the quote panel and the source line stack down the right column, with the source right-aligned inside the white card at its bottom-right; there is no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

One large 3D illustration: a spiral of glossy, inflated cloud-shaped discs (Salesforce cloud motif) sweeping from the bottom-left up and round to the top-centre. Colour runs from deep cobalt blue at the bottom-left, through bright cyan and aqua on the left and lower-right, to pale lilac and pink at the top; the ring thins into small floating fragments toward the centre-left, with the discs seen edge-on on the lower right. Lighting is soft studio lighting with specular highlights on the disc edges; the illustration is cropped by the left and bottom slide edges, not boxed. Also present: three small white four-point sparkle icons at the right edge near the card (x 85.4% y 44.8%, x 89.2% y 41.6%, x 91.6% y 43.5%), a white Salesforce cloud logo top-right (x 90.5%, y 8.2%, 5.2 by 6.5) with the lowercase wordmark salesforce in white, and a white rounded card with a thin white border and a faint glow, containing a translucent white quote panel. The source line is a text element and no chart or table is present.

**Design**

Background is a flat pale sky blue #CFE9FE with no gradient or pattern. The headline 'Claims transformation can’t wait' is cobalt #022AC0 and 'any longer.' is bright sky blue #00B3FF. The call-out pill is solid cobalt #022AC0 with yellow #FCC003 text. The quote is navy #002775 in italic, with 65% in bold. The source is dark navy #001E5B. The headline face is a bold geometric grotesque in the Avant Garde Demi style (sans-serif, demi-bold weight); the quote is a humanist italic sans in the Salesforce Sans style; the source is Salesforce Sans bold label and regular text. Headline lines are about 8.3% of slide height (about 11.1% for 'any longer.'), the call-out is about 3.2%, the quote about 2.8% and the source about 1.4%. The pill has fully rounded ends with a corner radius of half its height. The card has a white outline about 0.3% of slide height thick with a soft drop glow. The quote panel is a translucent white rounded rectangle with a soft inner gradient, with corner radius about 1% of slide height.

### Slide 33

**Text**

Everything you need to start the
claim is already in the conversation
Authenticate
Understand the Loss
Reassure and Guide
Capture the Details

**Layout**

Centered composition on a light blue field. Two-line headline sits across the top, centered horizontally with its top near 12% of the slide height. Below it, a central circular photo cluster is flanked by two columns of two rounded pill cards each: left column (Authenticate, Reassure and Guide) and right column (Understand the Loss, Capture the Details). Each pill has its circular blue icon on the inner edge facing the photo. Salesforce cloud logo sits in the top-right corner. No footer, no page number, no source line.

**Visuals**

Four white-outlined rounded pill cards with translucent light fill; a large faint circular halo ring behind the centre; a saturated bright-blue (#00B3FF) circle behind a photograph of a woman cut out from her background; four dark-blue (#022AC0) circular badges each holding a white line icon: stopwatch (Authenticate, left top), handshake (Reassure and Guide, left bottom), magnifying glass over a dollar sign (Understand the Loss, right top), document with lines and a dollar mark (Capture the Details, right bottom). Photograph: a woman with shoulder-length dark hair wearing a grey-blue blazer over a white top, looking down with a concerned expression, seated at a grey laptop whose edge is visible at the bottom; she is shown from the waist up in a head-and-torso crop, cool soft studio lighting, dominant colours slate blue #5C7A99, white, dark brown hair, bright blue backdrop. Salesforce logo: light blue (#4BB0F5) cloud with white lowercase wordmark 'salesforce'.

**Design**

Background is a flat pale sky blue (#CFE9FE) with no gradient or pattern. Headline and labels are deep navy (#001E5B) in AvantGarde Demi style, a geometric sans-serif in a demi-bold weight, no italics, no capitals, normal letter-spacing. Headline is large at about 8.3% of slide height; labels are about 3.5% of slide height. Accents are cobalt #022AC0 for icon badges and bright blue #00B3FF for the photo disc. Pill cards are near-white (#EEF8FF) with a thin white stroke, fully rounded ends (radius equal to half the height), and a soft diffuse blue shadow beneath. Halo ring is white at low opacity with a soft glow. Icon glyphs are thin white line art. No hard shadows, no gradients on shapes.

### Slide 34

**Text**

What if
the phone call itself
could start the claim?

**Layout**

A single centred column with the three text lines stacked in the middle of the slide, the handshake icon centred below them, and the Salesforce logo in the top-right corner. Title lines are centred horizontally on the slide's vertical centre line, with about 28% top margin before the first line. There is no footer, slide number or separate title placeholder.

**Visuals**

One white line icon of a handshake, centred below the text, at about x 40.2%, y 70.0%, width 16.4%, height 16.7%. It is drawn with a uniform white stroke of about 0.6% of slide height, with open outlined cuffs at left and right and two clasped hands in the middle, with no fill. One Salesforce cloud logo at top right, a light blue cloud (#4FB3F5) with the white lowercase wordmark 'salesforce' inside. The backdrop is a blue gradient photograph-style image with a soft bright glow along the bottom edge. No chart, table or photograph of people is present.

**Design**

Background: Blue gradient, bright blue #0059F0 at top, deepening to #0026B0 in the middle and glowing to #009CFF at the bottom edge, with a base colour fill of #CFE9FE beneath it. Full-bleed saturated blue gradient background, brightest at top and bottom centre, darker in the middle-left and middle-right. Line 1 is in marigold yellow #FCC003 and lines 2 and 3 are in white #FFFFFF, all in a bold geometric sans-serif (AvantGarde Demi style), with title text at about 10.7% of slide height and no capitals beyond the first letter of 'What' and the first letter of the sentence. Lines are centred with generous leading. The handshake icon and logo are white and light blue respectively, with no shadows, boxes or corner radii.

### Slide 35

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Presents a four-part capability layout with short bold headings and supporting sentences that map onto the product flow.
- Worth keeping: Keep the editions slide 11 structure of paired bars with a value/price multiple, the problem framing on slide 20 with its single stat, and the clear title-plus-subtitle-plus-benefit-list pattern of slide 35.

**Text**

GA
Now
Customer-facing
Claims Service Customer Assistance
Automate claims intake with a voice-enabled agent.
Automate claims intake from first word to filed record
Capture incident details, retrieve policy history, and trigger
confirmation emails, all during the call, without manual entry, cutting
claim initiation time from the moment a policyholder speaks.
Deliver accuracy at every step of intake
Real-time validation, guided questioning, and a standardized voice flow
eliminate human error and ensure every claim is captured completely
and consistently — no follow-up calls required.
Give policyholders a faster, more personal experience
Offer 24/7 voice-guided claim initiation that knows who's calling,
retrieves their policy details instantly, and keeps them informed,
turning a stressful moment into a smooth, confident interaction.
Reduce FNOL costs without adding headcount
Deflect routine claims calls from live agents, eliminate rework from
incomplete intake, and free your team to focus on complex cases,
lowering administrative overhead while improving resource allocation.

Keypad

**Layout**

Two-column composition on a light blue field: a left text column (x 3.9% to about 44%) holds the title, subtitle and four heading-plus-body blocks stacked top to bottom, while a phone mockup sits in the right third (x about 63% to 83%). The title sits top-left below the GA Now badge at x 3.9%, y 13.9%, with the subtitle beneath it and a thin rule separating the header from the body. The Customer-facing pill sits top-right, the Salesforce cloud logo sits just below it, and there is no footer, no page number and no date. Left margin is about 3.9% and the text column ends by about 44%; the body text runs to about y 91.7%.

**Visuals**

Phone mockup (right third, x about 63.5% to 82.9%, y 27.7% to 96.7%): a smartphone screen shown head-on in dark teal-green (#0E3B36 to #17604F), set inside a pale white translucent rounded frame with a soft drop shadow (placed as a 1043 by 2048 px image at x 62.7%, y 28.2%, w 20.6%, h 71.8%). The screen shows an incoming call in progress at the top: time 6:48 at top-left, signal, Wi-Fi and battery icons at top-right, a large green round call button (#1FA968 approx) with a white handset icon, the caption CUSTOMER SUPPORT in small spaced capitals, the name Cumulus Customer Care in bold white, a timer pill reading a green dot and 6:04, and a green audio waveform. A faint bridge photograph in the background is tinted green and dimmed. Bottom controls are circular translucent grey buttons: Mute (microphone icon), Speaker (speaker icon), Message (speech bubble icon) in the top row, and Add Call (plus icon), End (large red round button #E8343E with a white hang-up handset icon) and Keypad (chevron-up icon) in the bottom row. A home indicator bar sits at the bottom. Faint, partly obscured background text (a ride-hailing style card with Pickup in 9 min, a Joshua name and a Green Royal Motorbike vehicle line) is visible behind the controls, and a large faded number sits behind the caller name. The phone image is listed twice in the file at the same position, which is one visual. Lighting is flat, frontal, with no visible highlights. Not cropped; the phone is fully shown. Three small glossy blue 3D four-point star sparkle icons (about #3FA9F5 with white highlights) sit beside the phone and in the body column: one at x 88.5%, y 33.8% (right of the phone), one at x 46.5%, y 55.8% (between the text column and the phone), and one at x 57.7%, y 71.0% (below and left of the phone). A Salesforce cloud logo (light blue cloud #4BB0F5 with white lowercase wordmark salesforce) sits at x 90.5%, y 8.2%. A thin light-blue horizontal rule (#8FD3FB, about 0.3% of slide height thick) runs from x 3.9% to about 43.7% at y about 29.8%. A dark-blue GA Now pill badge (#0A2FC0 to #022AC0 gradient, rounded ends) sits at top-left, with a light blue inner pill (#A9DDFB) holding the word Now. A dark-blue rounded pill (#0A2FC0 gradient) at top-right holds Customer-facing. No photographs in the body beyond the phone background.

**Design**

Background: Solid light sky blue #CFE9FE covering the entire slide. Light sky-blue flat background (#CFE9FE) with no gradient or pattern. Title in a bold geometric grotesque (AvantGardeDemiSFDC, close to ITC Avant Garde Demi, a sans-serif) in strong cobalt blue #022AC0, large, about 6.9% of slide height per capital-height estimate. Subtitle in the same demi sans in deep navy #002775, about 4.0% of slide height. Heading lines in the same demi sans in cobalt #022AC0 at about 2.35%. Body copy in SalesforceSans-Regular, a humanist sans, regular weight, in dark blue #023779, about 2.2% of slide height, with generous line spacing of about 3.1% of slide height between lines. Accent colours: cobalt #022AC0, light blue #8FD3FB rule, sky blue #3FA9F5 stars. Shapes are fully rounded pills with no visible shadow except a soft drop shadow under the GA Now badge. The phone frame is a rounded rectangle with about 5% corner radius and a soft shadow. The Customer-facing pill has a subtle shadow. No borders on text.

**Text**

Policy Administration
&

**Layout**

Two-column layout: a tall light-blue rounded panel with a concave right edge fills the left side from x 4.7% to 40.5%, holding an illustration cluster, while the title block is left-aligned at x 43.8% and vertically centred on the right. The three-line title sits above a single-line subtitle. Left margin is about 4.7%, the logo sits top right at 90.5% x and 8.2% y, and there is no footer, slide number or date.

**Visuals**

Illustration cluster on the light-blue panel, all 3D-rendered and glossy. A fuzzy bright blue round character with a black visor-style face and a small rounded ear sits at upper left, overlapping a large purple card that carries a white diagonal up-right arrow icon near its top right. A smaller purple rounded card sits to the lower right of the large card, with a clapping hands emoji in medium brown skin tone overlapping its top edge. A white translucent pill (opacity 0.40) with a purple four-point sparkle in a white circle sits across the lower part of the large card, and a solid white rounded field sits inside it. A purple horizontal bar with a lighter purple filled segment on its left (about 40% of its length) sits below the cards. Small sparkles: a yellow four-point sparkle upper left, a larger lilac four-point sparkle above the blue character, a small lilac sparkle lower left of the large card, and a cluster of two yellow sparkles above the clapping hands. A large glossy yellow four-point star is cropped at the left edge of the slide, overlapping the panel border. Lighting is soft frontal with specular highlights. The Salesforce cloud logo is at top right.

**Design**

Background is a deep violet-to-indigo gradient, brighter violet #7B22F0 at top centre fading to indigo #1E1A6B at bottom edges, with a soft lavender glow #8A5FD6 along the bottom centre. Title text is white #FFFFFF in a geometric grotesque bold (close to AvantGarde Demi), about 8.9% of slide height per line, tight leading. Subtitle is white #FFFFFF in a humanist sans regular (Salesforce Sans style), about 4.0% of slide height. Left panel is pale blue #CFE9FE with large rounded corners on the left and a concave curved right edge. Purple shapes are #5A1BA9 with rounded corners of about 15% of their height. Accent colours are yellow #FFC93C and lilac #C77DFF in the illustrations. No drop shadows on text; the cards have soft purple shadows beneath them.

### Slide 37

**Review notes**

- Review: The consequential weaknesses are legibility and density: slide 11 has a tiny itemised price list inside the bars, slides 38 and 41 to 44 rely on unreadable screenshots with thin labels, and slides 25, 27 and 37 carry very small text and callouts.

**Text**

GA Now / Claims Financial Authority / Eliminate authority leakage with automated Letter of Authority (LOA) enforcement / Salesforce / Validate every claims payment automatically against the user's configured financial authority (LOA) limit / Route over-threshold payments through a clear escalation chain: adjuster → supervisor → manager → director / Escalate high-value claims through multiple authority tiers at once, when needed / Configure unique financial authority limits for a user per policy coverage product and financial type / Trace every authorization to a named approver and be audit-ready for regulators / Screenshot UI text: Insurance Agent Console; Claim Coverage Payment Detail; Burglary Coverage 98aa6954; Pay CCPD; Cancel CCPD; Delete CCPD; Claim Coverage; Burglary Coverage; Work Guide; Route Approval Request; Approval Decision; Approve; Reject; Decision Comments; Finish; Related; Details; Information; Name; Claim Coverage; Claim Coverage Reserve Detail; Recipient; CP-00001737; Claim Payment Summary; Payment Status; Authority Approved; Benefit Name; Claimed Amount; USD 5,500.00; Activity; Upcoming & Overdue; No activities to show.; Get started by sending an email, scheduling a task, and more.; No past activity. Past meetings and tasks marked as done show up here.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a light blue field. The GA Now pill badge sits top-left at about 4% left margin and the Salesforce cloud logo sits top-right near the 4% right margin. The title is top-left below the badge, the subtitle sits directly beneath it, and a thin pale-blue rule separates the header from the body. The left column holds four left-aligned text paragraphs, and the right column holds a framed product screenshot vertically centred against the text. A small blue sparkle icon sits on the gutter between the columns. There is no footer, page number or source line.

**Visuals**

One framed screenshot of the Salesforce Lightning Insurance Agent Console, shown as a rounded-corner white device-style frame with a soft grey drop shadow, not full-bleed, boxed at about 49.5% to 96.5% horizontally and 31.7% to 81.7% vertically. The page shows a Burglary Coverage 98aa6954 Claim Coverage Payment Detail record with Pay CCPD, Cancel CCPD and Delete CCPD buttons at top right, a Work Guide card, a Route Approval Request form with Approve and Reject radio buttons and an empty Decision Comments box, a blue Finish button at right, and a Details tab listing Name, Claim Coverage, Claim Coverage Reserve Detail, Recipient CP-00001737, Claim Payment Summary, Payment Status Authority Approved, Benefit Name and Claimed Amount USD 5,500.00. The Activity panel on the right shows an empty state. The screenshot is mostly white and light grey with blue links and buttons. A blue four-point sparkle icon, about 2.3% by 4.2% of the slide, sits at about 46.5% to 48.8% horizontally and 55.8% to 60% vertically, straddling the column gutter, with a light blue gradient fill and no outline. A Salesforce cloud logo in mid-blue sits top-right. The GA Now pill badge sits top-left. Two further small images are present, one at the right edge of the screenshot top bar and one faint sparkle near the lower middle of the screenshot, both too small to read clearly.

### Slide 38

**Review notes**

- Review: The consequential weaknesses are legibility and density: slide 11 has a tiny itemised price list inside the bars, slides 38 and 41 to 44 rely on unreadable screenshots with thin labels, and slides 25, 27 and 37 carry very small text and callouts.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or crop the screenshots on slides 38 and 41 to 44 to the one relevant region and add a one-line takeaway per slide, and raise the minimum text size on slides 11, 12, 25 and 27.

**Text**

Other Key Innovations
salesforce
Claims Reserve Management
Set independent loss and expense reserve budgets at both the Claim and Claim Coverage levels during intake
Trigger automatic warnings when Coverage-level reserves exceed Claim-level reserves for the same reserve type
Maintain a full audit trail of every reserve adjustment for finance and compliance review
Deploy within existing Digital Insurance: Claims Management licensing – no new SKU needed
Insurance Setup Migration
Connect directly to a source org and resolve the scoped definition sets and entity graph
Deploy both standard definition sets and customer-configured custom entities into the target org
Sequence deployment in dependency-safe order, with full logging of successes, failures, and skipped items
Re-run deployments idempotently – no duplicate records created on repeat runs
Eliminate manual, error-prone recreation of complex insurance configurations across environments
External Policy Administration Support
Onboard policies from any external PAS via a purpose-built policy creation API – no data migration project required
Build native Insurance Policy, Coverage, Asset, and Participant records straight from external PAS data
Generate the product-specific JSON contract automatically from your configured product hierarchy
Invoke limit setup automatically, so downstream claims APIs (adjustments, standing, limits) work unchanged
Remove the #1 adoption blocker for carriers with existing policy infrastructure – no rip-and-replace, claims engine works exactly as it does today

**Layout**

Three equal columns of rounded cards sit side by side below a single title row, with the title left-aligned at the top left and the Salesforce cloud logo at the top right. Each card has a saturated blue header band holding a centred yellow heading and a white rounded body below it, with consistent outer margins of about 1.5 percent of slide width; there is no footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

None apart from the three blue card graphics and the Salesforce cloud logo at top right. The cards are rounded blue panels with white rounded body panels inside; beneath each card a faded, lighter blue reflection of the card runs off the bottom edge of the slide. The Salesforce logo is a light blue cloud (about #4DB0F5) with white lowercase 'salesforce' text.

**Design**

Background is pale sky blue #CFE9FE. Slide title is navy #001E5B in a bold geometric sans (AvantGardeDemiSFDC-style), about 6.7 percent of slide height. Card headings are yellow #FCC003 in the same bold sans, about 3.5 percent of slide height. Body text is navy #001E5B in a regular humanist sans (Salesforce Sans-style), about 2.5 percent of slide height, with round black-navy bullets. Card headers are blue around #0A3CF0 with a darker navy #001E8C edge; white body panels have large corner radii of about 2 percent of slide width and soft shadows. Reflections fade toward the bottom edge.

Group Benefits
Winter '27 Innovations

Two-zone horizontal split: a tall pale-blue rounded panel with a concave right edge occupies the left third (about x 4.7% to 40.5%, y 8.7% to 91.1%), with a 3D illustration cluster inside it; the title block sits in the right two-thirds, left-aligned at x 43.8%, with the title's baseline around y 67% and the subtitle at y 71.9%. The Salesforce cloud logo sits top right. No footer, page number or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Illustration: a cluster of stylised 3D objects. A blue fuzzy sphere with a black glossy visor-like shape (a character face with sunglasses) sits top-left over a large purple rounded card that has a light purple diagonal up-right arrow icon in its top-right corner. Below and right is a smaller purple rounded square with a brown 3D clapping hands emoji overlapping its top edge. A translucent white pill-shaped input bar with a white circular button containing a purple four-point sparkle sits across the lower middle of the purple card. A purple horizontal progress bar with a lighter purple filled segment (about 40% filled) sits beneath the cards. Small 3D sparkles: a yellow four-point star and a larger lilac four-point star above the sphere, a small yellow sparkle pair beside the clapping hands, a small lilac sparkle on the purple card, and a large glossy yellow four-point star cropped at the left edge of the slide, about x 0% to 4%, y 64% to 79%. Lighting is soft and frontal with glossy highlights on the 3D objects. Composition is centred within the light panel with the cluster occupying roughly x 0% to 33% and y 27% to 79%. Not cropped except the yellow star at the left edge. Dominant colours: light blue #CFE9FE panel, purple #5A1BA9 cards, vivid blue #2A7FE0 sphere, yellow #F7C52B stars, lilac #C58CF2 sparkles, brown #6B3A1E hands. Salesforce logo: cloud shape in sky blue #54B6F5 with white lowercase wordmark 'salesforce'.

**Design**

Background is a violet-to-indigo radial gradient, brightest violet #7A22E6 near the top centre, fading to deep indigo #2A1A8C at the lower edges, with a soft lilac glow #8A63D8 along the bottom centre. Title text is white #FFFFFF in a geometric sans-serif, bold-weight, like Avant Garde Demi, with approximately 8.9% slide-height size. Subtitle is white #FFFFFF in a humanist sans-serif regular weight, about 4% of slide height. Accent colours: purple #5A1BA9 and light blue #CFE9FE. The light panel has a rounded top-left and bottom-left corner radius of about 2% of slide width and a concave curved right edge. No shadows on text; the 3D objects cast soft shadows onto the panel.

### Slide 40

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Uses a two-column challenge and solution layout with matched bullets, so each problem is answered directly beside it.

**Text**

The Challenge
Group contracts and member / policies never change in isolation, / and quote, enrollment, and / servicing data sit in disconnected / steps
Plan assignments, overrides, and / census data get re-keyed by hand / at every stage, which is slow and / error-prone
Teams can't see the cost impact of / a change before committing, so / servicing takes hours and / reconciliation is manual
The Solution
Price and quote with precision: / Surcharges, external pricing engines / and per-member costs carry from / quote to policy
Carry census and plan data / forward: Auto-assign plans by group / class and clone census into / enrollment, keeping member / overrides
Service contracts consistently: / Endorsements and renewals create / versioned contracts and update / every member policy
Handle member life events: / Dependent, class and re-rate / changes run through versioned / policies and approvals
See the cost impact: Preview cost / before commit and reconcile / automatically
Cut group servicing from hours to minutes, with cost / visibility at every step

**Layout**

Two equal columns flank a large central circular photo, with the left column for The Challenge and the right column for The Solution, each under a pill-shaped title header at the top of its column. A full-width rounded panel with a pale white fill spans the middle band, and a blue banner pill centred along the bottom closes the composition with a horizontal arrow line extending from each side. The Salesforce cloud logo sits in the top-right corner, and no slide number or footer is visible.

**Visuals**

Full-colour photograph of a smiling young woman with short dark wavy hair, wearing a multicoloured patterned short-sleeved shirt with orange, green, blue and white motifs, looking down and holding a gold smartphone in both hands. She is framed from the head to the mid-thigh, cropped at the bottom edge of a circle, with a bright-blue radial gradient circle behind her (#0B3FFF at the top fading to about #021F8A at the bottom) and a thin white hairline ring inside the circle. The photo is lit evenly from the front. Behind the circle is a large pale white halo at about 50% opacity. Two dark-blue (#022AC0) rounded header pills carry the titles and a blue (#066AFE) rounded banner pill carries the closing line. Two thin dark-blue lines with an arrowhead on the right run to the left and right of the bottom banner. The Salesforce cloud logo is light blue with white lettering reading salesforce.

**Design**

Background: Solid pale sky blue #CFE9FE covering the whole slide. Pale sky-blue background #CFE9FE with a near-white rounded panel (#FFFFFF at about 85% and 60% opacity) behind each column. Titles are gold #F3BD29 in a bold geometric sans (AvantGarde Demi style) at about 3.2% of slide height, set on dark-blue #022AC0 pills. Body text is dark navy #001E5B in SalesforceSans Regular at about 2.5% of slide height, with bold lead-ins in SalesforceSans Bold. Bullets are small navy dots. The closing line is white AvantGarde Demi at about 3.0% of slide height on the #066AFE banner. Shapes have fully rounded (pill) ends and soft shadows; the circle has a blue gradient.

### Slide 44

**Review notes**

- Review: The consequential weaknesses are legibility and density: slide 11 has a tiny itemised price list inside the bars, slides 38 and 41 to 44 rely on unreadable screenshots with thin labels, and slides 25, 27 and 37 carry very small text and callouts.
- Review: The Group Benefits run (slides 41 to 44) repeats one template with little explanation, so the feature story fades into screenshot tours, and a few slides, such as the pricing tiles on 10 and 12 and the agenda-style dividers, are packed or filler-like.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or crop the screenshots on slides 38 and 41 to 44 to the one relevant region and add a one-line takeaway per slide, and raise the minimum text size on slides 11, 12, 25 and 27.

**Visuals**

Photograph-style illustration of a silver-white desktop monitor (front view, slightly rounded bezel, thin stand narrowing to a base) with a blue glow reflection under the stand. The monitor screen shows a Salesforce Lightning Opportunities Kanban board titled All Commercial Submissions, with a navy top bar, pipeline stage chevrons Prospect (0), Submission (2) at $28,000, Review (2) at $14,500, Underwrite (3) at $104,420, Proposal/Quote (0), Bound (0) and Closed Won (0). (Silva Industries, Levin Insurance Agency, 68). Other screenshot elements: Salesforce cloud logo, app launcher, global search field Search Salesforce, tabs Opportunities, Silva Industries - Ne..., Silva Industries, Levin Insurance Ag..., buttons New and Printable View, and a Search this list... field. Dominant screen colours are white, pale grey and navy #1B4F8C for stage chevrons with green #2E844A dollar values. Lighting is flat and even, no shadow except the soft blue glow beneath the stand. Monitor is full, unCropped and sits on a stand; no overlay. Five cyan sparkle icons and a blue Salesforce cloud logo are placed around the monitor.

**Design**

Background light sky blue #CFE9FE with no gradient or texture. Title and subtitle text is navy #001E5B; headings are navy #001E5B in bold sans; body text is dark blue #002775 in regular sans. Accent pink #FF538A on the Coming soon tag with white italic bold text; sparkles and cloud in cyan-blue #2BB5F0 and #1B96FF. ### Slide 47

**Text**

Agentic Insurance Underwriting
Coming soon!
End-to-end capabilities across the underwriting value chain
Client & Distribution / Engagement
Value Analysis
Lead / Opportunity
Process Intelligence
Business / Value Drivers
Process Mapper
Account 360
Data Ingestion & Extraction
Digital Insurance
Data Ingestion / & Unification
Reconciliation & Unification
Quoting / & Binding
External PAS
Workflow / Orchestration
Stage Management
OOTB Integrations
Work Management
Integrations
Integration Hub
LOB templates
Submission Data Model
Platform Foundations
Agentic Insurance Underwriting

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at about 3.5% from the top with the subtitle beneath it, and a small Salesforce cloud logo at top-right beside a 'Coming soon!' badge in the top-right corner. Six rounded-rectangle panels form a loose ring around a central circular diagram: Client & Distribution Engagement (top-left), Business Value Drivers (top-right), Data Ingestion & Unification (middle-left), Quoting & Binding (middle-right), Workflow Orchestration (bottom-left) and Integrations (bottom-right), with a wide Platform Foundations panel centred along the bottom edge. Text is left-aligned in the side panels with labels right-aligned on the left of the ring and left-aligned on the right of the ring; no footer or page number is visible.

**Visuals**

A circular diagram centred on the slide: a thin bright-blue (#0A2FD6-ish) ring with 15 dark navy (#001E5B) node dots spaced around it, each with a white centre, each labelled. Clockwise from top: Value Analysis, Process Intelligence, Process Mapper, Digital Insurance, External PAS, OOTB Integrations, Integration Hub, Submission Data Model, LOB templates, Work Management, Stage Management, Reconciliation & Unification, Data Ingestion & Extraction, Account 360, Lead / Opportunity. At the centre is a large dark navy (#001E5B) disc with a thin white inner ring, containing a white circular photo of a smiling woman with shoulder-length auburn/red hair, wearing a yellow blazer and a necklace, in head-and-shoulders framing, facing the camera, lit evenly with soft light on a white background; the photo is cropped to a circle and overlaps the bottom of the disc. Across the lower part of the disc sits a blue (#022AC0) ribbon banner with notched ends and a soft drop shadow, reading 'Agentic Insurance Underwriting' in white Arial Bold on two lines. Top-right has a light-blue (#00B3FF) rounded badge reading 'Coming soon!' in white bold italic. A Salesforce cloud logo in lighter blue sits to the right of the title area. Six pale translucent panels with white outlines and soft rounded corners provide the category backgrounds.

**Design**

Background: Solid pale blue #CFE9FE, covering the whole slide. Pale sky-blue background (#CFE9FE) with a subtle lighter gradient in the panels. Titles and category headings are in strong blue #022AC0 (panel headings) and navy #001E5B (main title, subtitle and node labels). Accents are the bright blue #00B3FF badge and the #022AC0 banner. Main title is an AvantGarde Demi-style geometric sans-serif, bold, about 6.7% of slide height. Subtitle is Salesforce Sans Regular, about 4.4% of slide height. Panel headings are AvantGarde Demi, about 3.2% of slide height. Node labels are Salesforce Sans Bold, about 2.0% of slide height. Panels have 1 pt white borders, rounded corners of roughly 1% of slide width, and no visible shadow. Ring line is a solid blue stroke. Banner has a soft drop shadow.

### Slide 48

**Text**

Insurance Underwriting Agentic Investments
Ambient Agents
Coming soon!
Conversational Agents
Future
Submission Intake
Extract data from structured documents
(ACORDs) and unstructured sources
(emails, SOVs, loss reports) into a
unified Submission Data Model
Accelerates Submission readiness
Product Fit
Find relevant products from the org
basis structural match, attributes,
coverage density and coverage attribute
fit with configurable weights
Instant product-match recommendation
Normaliser
Reconcile data from multiple sources
and formats into a consistent structure,
surfacing lineage so reviewers can see
where each value originated
Ensures single source of truth
Underwriting Brief
Generates a living snapshot of where
the submission stands, data summary
plus activity status so that UW never has
to piece together the picture manually
Eliminates context-switching
Admin Assistant
Helps admins build and modify stage
modules, activities, and transitions
through natural language - translating
intent into metadata API calls
Directly impacts time to value
Underwriter Assistant
Chat assistant with access to claims,
policies, stages, activities; Answers
questions with data-backing; Completes
manual activities with approval
Reduces time to decision
Document Insights
Surfaces key risk indicators, coverage gaps, and underwriting-relevant findings from
attached documents (ACORD 125/140, loss runs)
Unlocks trapped document knowledge

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a single pale blue field: a 2-column grid of Ambient Agents cards on the left (Submission Intake and Product Fit, Normaliser and Underwriting Brief, then full-width Document Insights below), separated by a vertical dotted divider at about x 58.6% from a single right column holding Conversational Agents cards (Admin Assistant, then Underwriter Assistant). The title sits top-left at x 4.7%, y 9.3%, spanning about 82% of the width, with the Salesforce cloud logo top-right at x 90.5%, y 8.2%; left margin about 8.7%, no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Non-text visuals: (1) Salesforce cloud logo, light blue #55B7F7 cloud with white lowercase 'salesforce' wordmark, top-right corner. (2) Two gradient pill section banners: Ambient Agents, a dark-to-mid blue gradient (#0B5FCC at left to #2A1F7A at right) with white outline; Conversational Agents, a navy-to-blue gradient (#2A1F7A to #0B5FCC) with white outline. (3) A decorative cluster of small four-point sparkle/plus signs in purple #8A2BE2, navy #001E5B and light lavender, between the Ambient Agents pill and the Coming soon! badge. (4) Two bright blue #00AEFF rounded badges: Coming soon! and Future, italic white text. (5) A small speech-bubble icon, gradient purple #7B4FBF to pink #D9467A, containing three white vertical bars, beside the Conversational Agents pill. (6) Seven white rounded header tabs and seven pale blue-to-white gradient card bodies with soft edges, each card body a light blue-white gradient panel (#E8F4FE to #FFFFFF). (7) Seven solid #8CCFFE footer bars under the card bodies. (8) A vertical dotted line in mid blue #5A8FD0 at x about 58.6%, y 12% to 97%, 1 pt dots. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background light sky blue #CFE9FE, flat with no gradient. Titles and body text navy #001E5B. Accents: blue gradient pills (#0B5FCC to #2A1F7A), bright cyan badges #00AEFF, footer bars #8CCFFE, white header tabs #FFFFFF. Title typeface is a geometric grotesque in demi-bold, close to Avant Garde Demi, sans-serif, at about 6.9% of slide height, roughly 1.1 times body size. Card headings are the same family at about 2.2% of slide height (medium-bold). Body text is a humanist sans, Salesforce Sans Regular, at about 2.0% of slide height, regular weight, line spacing about 1.35. Badge and italic labels are Salesforce Sans Bold Italic at about 1.7%. Cards: white rounded-end header tabs with fully rounded corners, card bodies square-cornered, no visible shadows, no outlines on body panels; pill banners have white stroke and soft drop shadow.

### Slide 49

**Text**

Thank / you

**Layout**

Centred composition on a full-bleed blue backdrop. The two-line word 'Thank / you' sits left of centre, offset so 'you' is indented right of 'Thank'. The Salesforce mascot stands at the right, overlapping the end of 'Thank'. Small yellow sparkles and a blue orb sit at the left of the word. No title placeholder, footer, slide number or margins are visible.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed blue gradient background image (1920 by 1080). A small blue spherical character with black sunglasses at about x 26 to 40 percent, y 46 to 70 percent, overlapping the left of 'you'. Three yellow four-point sparkles: one about 25 to 27.5 percent x, 52 to 58 percent y (small); one about 26 to 30 percent x, 45 to 50 percent y; one about 71 to 75 percent x, 28 to 35 percent y (small, top right of the mascot); and a larger one at about 75 to 78 percent x, 28 to 35 percent y. A 3D rendered Salesforce Astro mascot in a brown furry hood costume with a dark brown face, black eyes and brown hair, standing with its feet at about y 80 percent, with its arms holding a light blue Salesforce cloud logo reading 'salesforce' in white lowercase, which sits at about 66 to 77 percent x, 60 to 75 percent y. Soft shadow beneath the mascot's feet. Lighting is even studio light from above on the blue backdrop, with a lighter cyan glow along the bottom edge.

**Design**

Background is a blue radial-to-vertical gradient: deep royal blue #0050F0 at the top, deeper blue #0022A8 in the middle and left, with a light cyan-blue glow #0A9CFF at the bottom edge. Title text 'Thank you' is white #FFFFFF (with 'Thank' #FFFEFE). Only accent colours are yellow sparkles #FFD200 and the Salesforce cloud blue #00A1E0. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif resembling AvantGarde Demi (AvantGardeDemiSFDC), medium-bold weight, lowercase-style rounded letterforms, very large, about 24.6 percent of slide height. No borders, shapes or corner radii; the only shadow is the soft ground shadow under the mascot.
